Let me look at the exact state of things more carefully. The `_review_grouping_key` function currently returns `None` for ALL calls (both review and non-review detectors). The issue is that when `_review_grouping_key` returns None (which is now always), it falls through to the next grouping functions, and for review findings specifically, it eventually hits `f"detector::{detector}"` which gives `detector::review`. This means review findings still get auto-clustered.

The fix needed: In `_grouping_key`, explicitly return None for review/subjective_review detectors BEFORE the chain of or-expressions. Or change `_review_grouping_key` to return a sentinel that causes early exit.

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested implementation of a detailed 14-step plan titled "Epic Synthesis: Continuous LLM-Driven Meta-Plan." This adds an LLM-driven meta-planning system to the desloppify codebase that:
   - Continuously maintains epics (root-cause clusters with theses) from review findings
   - Injects `synthesis::pending` as a T1 queue item when review findings drift from last synthesis
   - Dismisses contradictory/false-positive findings
   - Orders work by dependency
   - Produces a strategy summary shown on every `next` and scan output
   - Stops auto-clustering review findings (they're now epic synthesis territory)
   - Provides a `plan synthesize` CLI subcommand

   After implementation, the user also requested: "Once you're done can you actually try it yourself to see if it works? make a stale dimensions and see if it pops up, then try to do then plan - see if you have the tools you need to actually understanding, update, edit, it, etc. - and see if you're guided through this process nicely"

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Living plan system: `PlanModel` with queue_order, clusters, skipped, overrides, superseded
   - Snapshot hash: MD5 hash of sorted open review/concerns finding IDs — single source of truth for synthesis staleness
   - Sync pattern: `sync_*()` functions in `stale_dimensions.py` that inject/prune synthetic queue items
   - Epic synthesis: LLM reads all findings + existing epics, produces root-cause clusters with theses
   - `synthesized_out` skip kind: findings dismissed by synthesis that can be re-evaluated on next synthesis
   - Work queue: `build_work_queue()` in `core.py` builds ranked items with plan ordering
   - Auto-clustering: `auto_cluster_findings()` groups findings by detector/dimension — review findings now excluded
   - Reconciliation: `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` cleans up dead findings from plan structures

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`** (MODIFIED)
     - Core schema file — extended with Epic types and plan version bump
     - Added `VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS`, `EPIC_PREFIX`, `Epic` TypedDict, `EpicSynthesisMeta` TypedDict
     - Extended `PlanModel` with `epics: dict[str, Epic]` and `epic_synthesis_meta: EpicSynthesisMeta`

     ```python
     VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = frozenset({
         "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
         "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
     })
     EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"

     class Epic(TypedDict, total=False):
         name: Required[str]
         thesis: Required[str]
         direction: Required[str]
         root_cause: str
         finding_ids: list[str]
         supersedes: list[str]
         dismissed: list[str]
         agent_safe: bool
         dependency_order: int
         action_steps: list[str]
         source_clusters: list[str]
         status: str
         created_at: str
         updated_at: str
         synthesis_version: int
         auto: bool

     class EpicSynthesisMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
         synthesized_ids: list[str]
         last_run: str
         version: int
         dismissed_ids: list[str]
         finding_snapshot_hash: str
         strategy_summary: str
         trigger: str
     ```

     - Follows exact same pattern as existing `sync_unscored_dimensions()` / `sync_stale_dimensions()`
     ```python
     SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"

     def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
         findings = state.get("findings", {})
         ids = sorted(
             fid for fid, f in findings.items()
             if f.get("status") == "open"
             and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
         )
         if not ids:
             return ""
         return hashlib.md5("|".join(ids).encode()).hexdigest()

     def sync_synthesis_needed(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
         # Injects synthesis::pending at front of queue when hash drifts
         # Prunes when hash matches or no review findings exist
     ```

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`** (MODIFIED)
     - Added synthesis sync functions following exact same pattern as existing stale/unscored syncs
     - Added `SYNTHESIS_ID`, `SynthesisSyncResult`, `review_finding_snapshot_hash()`, `sync_synthesis_needed()`
     ```python
     SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
     
     @dataclass
     class SynthesisSyncResult:
         injected: bool = False
         pruned: bool = False
         @property
         def changes(self) -> int:
             return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
     
     def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
         findings = state.get("findings", {})
         ids = sorted(
             fid for fid, f in findings.items()
             if f.get("status") == "open"
             and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
         )
         if not ids:
             return ""
         return hashlib.md5("|".join(ids).encode()).hexdigest()
     
     def sync_synthesis_needed(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
         ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
         result = SynthesisSyncResult()
         order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
         current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
         meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
         last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
         has_synthesis = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
         if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
             if not has_synthesis:
                 order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
                 result.injected = True
         else:
             if has_synthesis:
                 order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
                 result.pruned = True
         return result
     ```

     - Added `build_synthesis_item()` — creates synthetic T1 queue item for pending synthesis

     ```python
     def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
         from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID, review_finding_snapshot_hash
         order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
         if SYNTHESIS_ID not in order:
             return None
         # ... counts review findings, computes trigger reason
         return {
             "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
             "kind": "synthesis_needed",
             "tier": 1, "effective_tier": 1,
             "confidence": "high", "detector": "synthesis",
             "summary": f"Meta-plan update: {total} review findings need integration into epics",
             "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",

         }
     ```

     ```python
                 dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
             return (-1, dep_order, 0, item.get("id", ""))
         if kind == "cluster":

     ```

```
   112→                file=sys.stderr,
   113→            )
   114→            return
   115→
   116→    plan = load_plan()
   117→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   118→    if not finding_ids:
   119→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   123→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   124→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   125→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   126→
   127→        state_file = state_path(args)
   128→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   129→        status = "wontfix" if kind == "permanent" else "false_positive"
   130→        resolved: list[str] = []
   131→        for fid in finding_ids:
   132→            resolved.extend(
   133→                state_mod.resolve_findings(
   134→                    state_data, fid, status, note or "", attestation=attestation
   135→                )
   136→            )
   137→        if resolved:
   138→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   139→
   140→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   141→    count = skip_items(
   142→        plan,
   143→        finding_ids,
   144→        kind=kind,
   145→        reason=reason,
   146→        note=note,
   147→        attestation=attestation,
   148→        review_after=review_after,
   149→        scan_count=scan_count,
   150→    )
   151→    save_plan(plan)
   152→
   153→    label = {"temporary": "Skipped", "permanent": "Wontfixed", "false_positive": "Marked false positive"}
   154→    print(colorize(f"  {label[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   155→    if review_after:
   156→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   157→
   158→
   159→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   160→    """Unskip findings — bring back to queue."""
   161→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   162→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   163→        return
   164→
   165→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   166→
   167→    plan = load_plan()
   168→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   169→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   170→    if not finding_ids:
   171→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
   175→    save_plan(plan)
   176→
   177→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   178→    if need_reopen:
   179→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   180→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   181→
   182→        state_file = state_path(args)
   183→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   184→        reopened: list[str] = []
   185→        for fid in need_reopen:
   186→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, fid, "open"))
   187→        if reopened:
   188→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   189→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s) in state.", "dim"))
   190→
   191→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   192→
   193→
   194→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   195→# Reopen
   196→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   197→
   198→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   199→    """Reopen resolved findings from plan context."""
   200→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   201→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   202→
   203→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   204→
   205→    state_file = state_path(args)
   206→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   207→
   208→    reopened: list[str] = []
   209→    for pattern in patterns:
   210→        reopened.extend(
   211→            state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, pattern, "open")
   212→        )
   213→
   214→    if not reopened:
   215→        print(colorize("  No resolved findings matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   216→        return
   217→
   218→    state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   219→
   220→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   221→    plan = load_plan()
   222→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   223→    count = 0
   224→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   225→    for fid in reopened:
   226→        if fid in skipped:
   227→            skipped.pop(fid)
   228→            count += 1
   229→        if fid not in order:
   230→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   231→            order.add(fid)
   232→            count += 1
   233→    if count:
   234→        save_plan(plan)
   235→
   236→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s).", "green"))
   237→    if count:
   238→        print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   239→
   240→
   241→def cmd_plan_done(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   242→    """Mark findings as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   243→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.selection import (
   244→        show_attestation_requirement,
   245→        validate_attestation,
   246→    )
   247→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
   248→
   249→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   250→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   251→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   252→
   253→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   254→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   255→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan done", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   256→        return
   257→
   258→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   259→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   260→        status="fixed",
   261→        patterns=patterns,
   262→        note=note,
   263→        attest=attestation,
   264→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   265→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   266→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   267→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   268→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   269→    )
   270→
   271→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd import cmd_resolve
   272→
   273→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   274→
   275→
   276→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   277→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   278→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   279→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   280→
   281→    plan = load_plan()
   282→    if clear_flag:
   283→        clear_focus(plan)
   284→        save_plan(plan)
   285→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   286→        return
   287→
   288→    if not cluster_name:
   289→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   290→        if active:
   291→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   292→        else:
   293→            print("  No active focus.")
   294→        return
   295→
   296→    try:
   297→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   298→    except ValueError as ex:
   299→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   300→        return
   301→    save_plan(plan)
   302→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   303→
   304→
   305→__all__ = [
   306→    "cmd_plan_describe",
   307→    "cmd_plan_done",
   308→    "cmd_plan_focus",
   309→    "cmd_plan_note",
   310→    "cmd_plan_reopen",
   311→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   312→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   313→]
   314→
```

```
1→"""Plan cluster subcommand handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→

    22→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    24→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    25→    action: str | None = getattr(args, "action", None)

    29→    except ValueError as ex:
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    31→        return
    32→    save_plan(plan)
    33→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    34→
    35→
    36→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    41→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")

    51→    except ValueError as ex:

    56→
    57→

    72→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)

   116→
   117→def _print_cluster_member(idx: int, fid: str, finding: dict | None) -> None:
   118→    """Print a single cluster member line with optional finding details."""
   119→    print(f"    {idx}. {fid}")
   120→    if not finding:
   121→        return
   122→    file = finding.get("file", "")
   123→    lines = finding.get("detail", {}).get("lines", [])
   124→    line_str = f" at lines: {', '.join(str(ln) for ln in lines)}" if lines else ""
   125→    if file:
   126→        print(colorize(f"       File: {file}{line_str}", "dim"))
   127→    summary = finding.get("summary", "")
   128→    if summary:
   129→        print(colorize(f"       {summary}", "dim"))
   130→
   131→
   132→def _load_findings_best_effort(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict:
   133→    """Load findings from state, returning empty dict on failure."""
   134→    rt = command_runtime(args)
   135→    return rt.state.get("findings", {})
   136→
   137→
   138→def _cmd_cluster_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   139→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   140→    plan = load_plan()
   141→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   142→    if cluster is None:
   143→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    # Header
   147→    auto_tag = "Auto-generated" if cluster.get("auto") else "Manual"
   148→    cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   149→    key_type = f" ({cluster_key.split('::', 1)[0]})" if cluster_key else ""
   150→    print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}", "bold"))
   151→    print(colorize(f"  Type: {auto_tag}{key_type}", "dim"))
   152→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   153→    if desc:
   154→        print(colorize(f"  Description: {desc}", "dim"))
   155→    action = cluster.get("action") or ""
   156→    if action:
   157→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {action}", "dim"))
   158→
   159→    # Members
   160→    finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   161→    if not finding_ids:
   162→        print(colorize("  Members: (none)", "dim"))
   163→    else:
   164→        findings = _load_findings_best_effort(args)
   165→        print(colorize(f"  Members ({len(finding_ids)}):", "dim"))
   166→        for idx, fid in enumerate(finding_ids, 1):
   167→            _print_cluster_member(idx, fid, findings.get(fid))
   168→
   169→    # Commands
   170→    print()
   171→    print(colorize("  Commands:", "dim"))
   172→    print(colorize(f'    Resolve all:  desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   173→    print(colorize(f"    Drill in:     desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   174→    print(colorize(f"    Skip:         desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   175→
   176→
   177→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   178→    plan = load_plan()
   179→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   180→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   181→    if not clusters:
   182→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   183→        return
   184→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   185→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   186→        member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   187→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   188→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   189→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   190→        auto_tag = " [auto]" if cluster.get("auto") else ""
   191→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{auto_tag}{desc_str}{marker}")
   192→
   193→
   194→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   195→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   196→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   197→    dispatch = {
   198→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   199→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   200→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   201→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   202→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   203→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   204→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   205→    }
   206→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   207→    if handler is None:
   208→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   209→        return
   210→    handler(args)
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   214→
```

```
1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    12→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    13→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    14→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    15→        print(f"  {fid}")
    16→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    17→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    18→
    19→
    20→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    21→    state: dict,
    22→    *,
    23→    status: str,
    24→    resolved_count: int,
    25→) -> None:
    26→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    27→        return
    28→    wontfix_count = sum(
    29→        1 for finding in state["findings"].values() if finding["status"] == "wontfix"
    30→    )
    31→    actionable = sum(
    32→        1
    33→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    34→        if finding["status"]
    35→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    36→    )
    37→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    38→    print(
    39→        colorize(
    40→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    41→            "yellow",
    42→        )
    43→    )
    44→    print(
    45→        colorize(
    46→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    47→            "dim",
    48→        )
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    53→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    54→        return ""
    55→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    56→
    57→
    58→def _print_score_movement(
    59→    *,
    60→    status: str,
    61→    prev_overall: float | None,
    62→    prev_objective: float | None,
    63→    prev_strict: float | None,
    64→    prev_verified: float | None,
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    67→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    68→) -> None:
    69→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    70→    if (
    71→        new.overall is None
    72→        or new.objective is None
    73→        or new.strict is None
    74→        or new.verified is None
    75→    ):
    76→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    77→        return
    78→
    79→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    80→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    81→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    82→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    83→    print(
    84→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    85→        + colorize(
    86→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    87→            "dim",
    88→        )
    89→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    90→        + colorize(
    91→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    92→        )
    93→    )
    94→    if target_strict is not None:
    95→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import _print_strict_target_nudge
    96→
    97→        _print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
    98→    if status == "wontfix":
    99→        strict_gap = round(new.overall - new.strict, 1)
   100→        if strict_gap > 0:
   101→            print(
   102→                colorize(
   103→                    f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
   104→                    f"Current gap: overall {new.overall:.1f} vs strict {new.strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
   105→                    "yellow",
   106→                )
   107→            )
   108→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
   109→        print(
   110→            colorize(
   111→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
   112→                "yellow",
   113→            )
   114→        )
   115→        print(
   116→            colorize(
   117→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
   118→                "dim",
   119→            )
   120→        )
   121→    elif status == "fixed":
   122→        print(
   123→            colorize(
   124→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
   125→                "yellow",
   126→            )
   127→        )
   128→
   129→
   130→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   131→    *,
   132→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   133→    state: dict,
   134→    all_resolved: list[str],
   135→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   136→) -> None:
   137→    has_review = any(
   138→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   139→        for fid in all_resolved
   140→    )
   141→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   142→        return
   143→
   144→    stale_dims = sorted(
   145→        dim
   146→        for dim in {
   147→            str(
   148→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   149→            ).strip()
   150→            for fid in all_resolved
   151→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   152→        }
   153→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   154→    )
   155→    if not stale_dims:
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   159→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   160→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   161→    print(
   162→        colorize(
   163→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   164→            "yellow",
   165→        )
   166→    )
   167→    print(
   168→        colorize(
   169→            "  Next subjective step: "
   170→            + f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(stale_dims)}`",
   171→            "dim",
   172→        )
   173→    )
   174→
   175→
   176→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   177→    remaining = sum(
   178→        1
   179→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   180→        if finding["status"] == "open"
   181→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   182→    )
   183→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   184→    if remaining > 0:
   185→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   186→        print(
   187→            colorize(
   188→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   189→                "dim",
   190→            )
   191→        )
   192→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   193→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   194→    print()
   195→    return next_command
   196→
```

```
   144→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   145→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   146→    config: dict[str, object],
   147→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   148→    lang: LangRun | None,
   149→) -> LangRun | None:
   150→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   151→    if not lang:
   152→        return None
   153→
   154→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   155→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   156→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   157→        lang,
   158→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   159→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   160→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   161→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   162→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   163→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   164→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   165→        ),
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   169→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   170→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   171→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   172→    }
   173→    return runtime_lang
   174→
   175→
   176→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   177→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   178→    *,
   179→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   180→) -> int:
   181→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   182→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   183→
   184→    reset_keys = {
   185→        key.strip()
   186→        for key in assessments
   187→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   188→    }
   189→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   190→
   191→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   192→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   193→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   194→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   195→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   196→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   197→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   198→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   199→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   200→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   201→            payload.pop("components", None)
   202→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   203→            continue
   204→        assessments[key] = {
   205→            "score": 0.0,
   206→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   207→            "assessed_at": now,
   208→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   209→            "placeholder": True,
   210→        }
   211→    return len(reset_keys)
   212→
   213→
   214→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   215→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   216→) -> int:
   217→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   218→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   219→
   220→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   221→    expired = 0
   222→    for payload in assessments.values():
   223→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   224→            continue
   225→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   226→            continue
   227→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   228→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   229→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   230→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   231→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   232→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   233→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   234→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   235→        payload.pop("components", None)
   236→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   237→        expired += 1
   238→    return expired

   241→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   242→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   243→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   244→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   245→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   246→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   247→    path = Path(args.path)
   248→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   249→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   250→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   251→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   252→        state
   253→    )
   254→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   255→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   256→            state,
   257→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   258→        )
   259→
   260→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   261→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   262→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   263→
   264→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   265→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   266→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   267→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   268→
   269→    return ScanRuntime(
   270→        args=args,
   271→        state_path=state_file,
   272→        state=state,
   273→        path=path,
   274→        config=config,
   275→        lang=lang,
   276→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   277→        profile=profile,
   278→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   279→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   280→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   281→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   282→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   283→    )
   284→
   285→
   286→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   287→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   288→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   289→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   290→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   291→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   292→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   293→        return findings
   294→
   295→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   296→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   297→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   298→    )
   299→    if not stale:
   300→        return findings
   301→
   302→    augmented = list(findings)
   303→    augmented.extend(stale)
   304→    for stale_finding in stale:
   305→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   306→    return augmented
   307→
   308→
   309→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   310→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   311→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   312→    *,
   313→    decay_scans: int,
   314→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   315→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   316→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   317→        findings,
   318→        state=runtime.state,
   319→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   320→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   321→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   322→    )
   323→
   324→
   325→def run_scan_generation(
   326→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   327→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   328→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   329→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   330→        disable_parse_cache,
   331→        enable_parse_cache,
   332→    )
   333→
   334→    enable_file_cache()
   335→    enable_parse_cache()
   336→    try:
   337→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   338→            runtime.path,
   339→            lang=runtime.lang,
   340→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   341→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   342→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   343→                profile=runtime.profile,
   344→            ),
   345→        )
   346→    finally:
   347→        disable_parse_cache()
   348→        disable_file_cache()
   349→
   350→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   351→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   352→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   353→    )
   354→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   355→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   356→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   357→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   358→    )
   359→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   360→        findings,
   361→        runtime,
   362→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   363→    )
   364→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   365→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   366→
   367→
   368→def merge_scan_results(
   369→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   370→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   371→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   372→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   373→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   374→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   375→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   376→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   377→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   378→
   379→    if not path_changed:
   380→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   381→    else:
   382→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   383→    prev_dim_scores = (
   384→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   385→    )
   386→
   387→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   388→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   389→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   390→
   391→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   392→
   393→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   394→        runtime.state,
   395→        findings,
   396→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   397→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   398→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   399→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   400→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   401→            potentials=potentials,
   402→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   403→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   404→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   405→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   406→        ),
   407→    )
   408→
   409→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   410→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   411→    )
   412→    state_mod.save_state(
   413→        runtime.state,
   414→        runtime.state_path,
   415→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   416→    )
   417→
   418→    # Clear needs_rescan flag after successful scan merge (best-effort)
   419→    if runtime.config.get("needs_rescan"):
   420→        try:
   421→            runtime.config["needs_rescan"] = False
   422→            _save_config(runtime.config)
   423→        except OSError:
   424→            pass
   425→
   426→    # Reconcile living plan + sync stale subjective dimensions
   427→    try:
   428→        from desloppify.engine.plan import (
   429→            load_plan,
   430→            reconcile_plan_after_scan,
   431→            save_plan,
   432→            sync_stale_dimensions,
   433→            sync_synthesis_needed,
   434→            sync_unscored_dimensions,
   435→        )
   436→
   437→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   438→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   439→        dirty = False
   440→
   441→        # Standard reconciliation (only if plan has user content)
   442→        if (
   443→            plan.get("queue_order")
   444→            or plan.get("overrides")
   445→            or plan.get("clusters")
   446→            or plan.get("skipped")
   447→        ):
   448→            recon = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, runtime.state)
   449→            if recon.changes:
   450→                dirty = True
   451→            if recon.resurfaced:
   452→                print(colorize(
   453→                    f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
   454→                    "cyan",
   455→                ))
   456→
   457→        # Unscored dimension sync (prepend to front, unconditional)
   458→        unscored_sync = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state)
   459→        if unscored_sync.changes:
   460→            dirty = True
   461→        if unscored_sync.injected:
   462→            print(colorize(
   463→                f"  Plan: {len(unscored_sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
   464→                "cyan",
   465→            ))
   466→
   467→        # Stale dimension sync (always — handles both cleanup and injection)
   468→        dim_sync = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state)
   469→        if dim_sync.changes:
   470→            dirty = True
   471→        if dim_sync.pruned:
   472→            print(colorize(
   473→                f"  Plan: {len(dim_sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   474→                "cyan",
   475→            ))
   476→        if dim_sync.injected:
   477→            print(colorize(
   478→                f"  Plan: {len(dim_sync.injected)} stale subjective dimension(s) queued for refresh.",
   479→                "cyan",
   480→            ))
   481→
   482→        # Synthesis sync (review findings changed → inject synthesis::pending)
   483→        synthesis_sync = sync_synthesis_needed(plan, runtime.state)
   484→        if synthesis_sync.changes:
   485→            dirty = True
   486→        if synthesis_sync.injected:
   487→            print(colorize(
   488→                "  Plan: review findings changed — epic synthesis needed.",
   489→                "cyan",
   490→            ))
   491→
   492→        # Auto-cluster findings into task groups
   493→        from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
   494→
   495→        ac_changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, runtime.state)
   496→        if ac_changes:
   497→            dirty = True
   498→
   499→        if dirty:
   500→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   501→    except Exception:
   502→        pass  # Plan reconciliation is best-effort
   503→
   504→    return ScanMergeResult(
   505→        diff=diff,
   506→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   507→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   508→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   509→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   510→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   511→    )
   512→
   513→
   514→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   515→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   516→    config: dict[str, object],
   517→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   518→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   519→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   520→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   521→    )
   522→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   523→    open_findings = [
   524→        finding
   525→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   526→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   527→        ).values()
   528→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   529→    ]
   530→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   531→        open_findings,
   532→        budget=noise_budget,
   533→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   534→    )
   535→
   536→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   537→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   538→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   539→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   540→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   541→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   542→    )
   543→
   544→
   545→def persist_reminder_history(
   546→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   547→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   548→) -> None:
   549→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   550→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   551→        return
   552→
   553→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   554→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   555→    state_mod.save_state(
   556→        runtime.state,
   557→        runtime.state_path,
   558→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   559→    )
   560→
   561→
   562→__all__ = [
   563→    "ScanStateContractError",
   564→    "ScanMergeResult",
   565→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   566→    "ScanRuntime",
   567→    "merge_scan_results",
   568→    "persist_reminder_history",
   569→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   570→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   571→    "run_scan_generation",
   572→]
   573→
```

```
   357→    if queue.get("items"):
   358→        return False
   359→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   360→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))

   389→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)

   416→
   417→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   418→    """Render an epic card."""
   419→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   420→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   421→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   422→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   423→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   424→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   425→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   426→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   427→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   428→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   429→
   430→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   431→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   432→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   433→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   434→    if root_cause:
   435→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   436→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   437→    if total_epics:
   438→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   439→    print()
   440→    if action_steps:
   441→        print("  Steps:")
   442→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   443→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   444→        print()
   445→    if finding_ids:
   446→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   447→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   448→        for fid in shown:
   449→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   450→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   451→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   452→        print()
   453→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   454→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   455→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   456→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   457→
   458→
   459→def render_terminal_items(
   460→    items: list[dict],
   461→    dim_scores: dict,
   462→    findings_scoped: dict,
   463→    *,
   464→    group: str,
   465→    explain: bool,
   466→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   467→    plan: dict | None = None,
   468→) -> None:
   469→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   470→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   471→
   472→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   473→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   474→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   475→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   476→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   477→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   478→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   479→
   480→    if group != "item":
   481→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   482→        return
   483→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   484→        if idx > 0:
   485→            print()
   486→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   487→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   488→
   489→        # Special rendering for synthesis and epic items
   490→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   491→            _render_synthesis_item(item)
   492→            continue
   493→        if kind == "epic":
   494→            _render_epic_item(item)
   495→            continue
   496→
   497→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   498→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   499→        elif len(items) > 1:
   500→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   501→        else:
   502→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   503→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   504→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   505→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   506→
   507→
   508→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   509→    if len(items) != 1:
   510→        return
   511→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   512→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   513→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   514→    if kind != "finding":
   515→        return
   516→    item = items[0]
   517→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   518→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   519→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   520→        primary = item.get(
   521→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   522→        )
   523→        print(f"    {primary}")
   524→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   525→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   526→        return
   527→
   528→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   529→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   530→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   531→        print(f"    {primary}")
   532→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   533→    else:
   534→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   535→
   536→    print(
   537→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   538→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   539→    )
   540→    print(
   541→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   542→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   543→    )
   544→
   545→
   546→def render_followup_nudges(
   547→    state: dict,
   548→    dim_scores: dict,
   549→    findings_scoped: dict,
   550→    *,
   551→    strict_score: float | None,
   552→    target_strict_score: float,
   553→) -> None:
   554→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   555→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   556→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   557→        state,
   558→        subjective_entries,
   559→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   560→        max_quality_items=3,
   561→        max_integrity_items=5,
   562→    )
   563→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   564→    if strict_score is not None:
   565→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   566→        if gap > 0:
   567→            print(
   568→                colorize(
   569→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   570→                    "cyan",
   571→                )
   572→            )
   573→        else:
   574→            print(
   575→                colorize(
   576→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   577→                    "green",
   578→                )
   579→            )
   580→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   581→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   582→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   583→
   584→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   585→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   586→        findings_scoped
   587→    )
   588→    parts: list[str] = []
   589→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   590→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   591→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   592→    open_review = [
   593→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   594→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   595→    ]
   596→    if low_dims:
   597→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   598→    if stale_count:
   599→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   600→    if unassessed_count:
   601→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   602→    if len(open_review):
   603→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   604→    if coverage_open > 0:
   605→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   606→
   607→    if parts:
   608→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   609→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   610→
   611→
   612→__all__ = [
   613→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   614→    "render_followup_nudges",
   615→    "render_queue_header",
   616→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   617→    "render_terminal_items",
   618→    "scorecard_subjective",
   619→    "show_empty_queue",
   620→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   621→]
   622→
```

```
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""

    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)

    85→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

   127→        dispatch = {

   132→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   133→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   134→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   135→        }
   136→        dispatch[plan_action](args)

   141→

   144→

   147→
   148→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
    40→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    41→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    42→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    46→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    47→        plan_data = plan
    48→
    49→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster
    50→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    51→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    52→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    53→        if active_cluster:
    54→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    55→
    56→    queue = build_work_queue(
    57→        state,
    58→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    59→            tier=tier_filter,
    60→            count=None,
    61→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    62→            status="open",
    63→            include_subjective=True,
    64→            plan=plan_data,
    65→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
    66→            cluster=effective_cluster,
    67→            collapse_clusters=True,
    68→        ),
    69→    )
    70→    items = queue.get("items", [])
    71→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    72→
    73→    # Header
    74→    total = len(items)
    75→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    76→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    77→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    78→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    79→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    80→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts})", "bold"))
    81→    else:
    82→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    83→
    84→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    85→    if focus:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    87→
    88→    if not include_skipped and skipped_count == 0:
    89→        # Check plan for skipped items not in queue
    90→        plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    91→        if plan_skipped_count:
    92→            print(colorize(
    93→                f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    94→                " hidden \u2014 use --include-skipped)",
    95→                "dim",
    96→            ))
    97→
    98→    if not items:
    99→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    # Determine which items to show
   103→    display_items = items
   104→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   105→        display_items = items[:top]
   106→
   107→    # Build rows
   108→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   109→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   110→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   111→        pos = str(idx)
   112→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   113→
   114→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   115→            tier_str = "T1"
   116→            detector = "synthesis"
   117→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   118→            n = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   119→            summary = f"[synthesis] {n} findings need integration"
   120→            cluster_name = ""
   121→        elif kind == "epic":
   122→            tier_str = "T1"
   123→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   124→            direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   125→            fcount = len(detail.get("finding_ids", []))
   126→            status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   127→            detector = "epic"
   128→            summary = f"[{direction}] [{fcount}] [{status}] {detail.get('thesis', '')}"
   129→            cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   130→        elif kind == "cluster":
   131→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
   132→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   133→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   134→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   135→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   136→        else:
   137→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   138→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   139→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   140→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   141→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   142→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   143→
   144→        suffix = ""
   145→        if item.get("plan_skipped"):
   146→            suffix = " [skip]"
   147→
   148→        summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
   149→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   150→
   151→    print()
   152→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   153→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   154→
   155→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   156→        remaining = len(items) - top
   157→        print(colorize(
   158→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   159→        ))
   160→    print()
   161→
   162→
   163→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
```

```
    40→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    41→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""

    58→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    59→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""

    69→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    70→    )
    71→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    72→    prev_subjective_scores = {

   162→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
```

```
1→"""Handler for ``plan synthesize`` subcommand."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.core.io.api import colorize
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.epic_synthesis import (
    11→    SynthesisDeps,
    12→    build_synthesis_prompt,
    13→    collect_synthesis_input,
    14→    synthesize_epics,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
    17→
    18→
    19→def cmd_plan_synthesize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    20→    """Run LLM epic synthesis to create/update the meta-plan."""
    21→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    22→    state = runtime.state
    23→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    24→        return
    25→
    26→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    27→    plan = load_plan()
    28→
    29→    if dry_run:
    30→        si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
    31→        prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
    32→        print(colorize("  Epic synthesis — dry run", "bold"))
    33→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    34→        print(f"  Open review findings: {len(si.open_findings)}")
    35→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
    36→        print(f"  New since last: {len(si.new_since_last)}")
    37→        print(f"  Resolved since last: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
    38→        print(colorize("\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:", "dim"))
    39→        print()
    40→        print(prompt)
    41→        return
    42→
    43→    # Without an LLM integration, show instructions for manual synthesis
    44→    result = synthesize_epics(
    45→        plan,
    46→        state,
    47→        deps=None,  # No LLM — dry-run mode
    48→        dry_run=True,
    49→        trigger="manual",
    50→    )
    51→
    52→    print(colorize("  Epic synthesis", "bold"))
    53→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    54→    print(colorize(
    55→        "  Note: LLM integration requires a configured LLM backend.",
    56→        "yellow",
    57→    ))
    58→    print(colorize(
    59→        "  Use --dry-run to see the synthesis prompt, then paste it into",
    60→        "dim",
    61→    ))
    62→    print(colorize(
    63→        "  your LLM of choice and import the result.",
    64→        "dim",
    65→    ))
    66→    print(f"\n  {result.strategy_summary}")
    67→
    68→
    69→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_synthesize"]
    70→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse

     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod

    16→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,

    19→    QueueBuildOptions,
    20→    build_work_queue,

    22→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    23→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness, check_tool_staleness
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    26→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    76→    if config_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→
    79→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    80→
    81→
    82→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    83→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    84→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    85→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    86→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    87→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    88→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    89→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    90→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    91→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    92→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    93→
    94→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    95→
    96→    # Load the living plan
    97→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    98→    plan = load_plan()
    99→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   100→    if (
   101→        plan.get("queue_order")
   102→        or plan.get("overrides")
   103→        or plan.get("clusters")
   104→    ):
   105→        plan_data = plan
   106→
   107→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   109→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   110→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   111→        if active_cluster:
   112→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   113→
   114→    queue = build_work_queue(
   115→        state,
   116→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   117→            tier=tier,
   118→            count=count,
   119→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   120→            scope=scope,
   121→            status=status,
   122→            include_subjective=True,
   123→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   124→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   125→            explain=explain,
   126→            plan=plan_data,
   127→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   128→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   129→        ),
   130→    )
   131→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   132→
   133→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   134→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   135→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   136→        state,
   137→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   141→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   142→    )
   143→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   144→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   145→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   147→        state,
   148→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   149→    )
   150→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   151→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   152→    ]
   153→    write_query(payload)
   154→
   155→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   156→    if output_file:
   157→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   158→            output_file,
   159→            payload,
   160→            len(items),
   161→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   162→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   163→        ):
   164→            return
   165→        raise SystemExit(1)
   166→
   167→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   171→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   172→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   176→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   177→        state.get("findings", {}),
   178→        state.get("scan_path"),
   179→    )
   180→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   183→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   184→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   186→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   187→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   188→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   189→    )
   190→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   191→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   192→        state,
   193→        dim_scores,
   194→        findings_scoped,
   195→        strict_score=strict_score,
   196→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   197→    )
   198→    print()
   199→
   200→
   201→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
```

```
   195→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   196→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   197→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   198→    state = runtime.state
   199→    config = runtime.config
   200→
   201→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   202→        return
   203→
   204→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
   205→    if stale_warning:
   206→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
   207→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   208→    if skill_warning:
   209→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   210→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   211→    if config_warning:
   212→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   213→
   214→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
   215→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
   216→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   217→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   218→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   219→        return
   220→
   221→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   222→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   223→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   224→        state,
   225→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   226→    )
   227→
   228→    # --- Entity resolution: classify what the user asked for ---
   229→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
   230→
   231→    # Dispatch: concerns special view
   232→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
   233→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
   234→        return
   235→
   236→    # Dispatch: subjective dimension
   237→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
   238→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    # Dispatch: mechanical dimension
   242→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
   243→        matches = _load_dimension_findings(state, entity, status_filter)
   244→        if not matches:
   245→            _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
   246→            return
   247→        pattern = entity.display_name
   248→    else:
   249→        # All other kinds: existing load_matches path
   250→        matches = load_matches(
   251→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    if not matches:
   255→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
   256→        return
   257→
   258→    (
   259→        surfaced_matches,
   260→        hidden_by_detector,
   261→        noise_budget,
   262→        global_noise_budget,
   263→        budget_warning,
   264→    ) = resolve_noise(
   265→        config,
   266→        matches,
   267→    )
   268→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   269→
   270→    payload = build_show_payload(
   271→        surfaced_matches,
   272→        pattern,
   273→        status_filter,
   274→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   275→            total_matches=len(matches),
   276→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   277→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   278→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   279→        ),
   280→    )
   281→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   282→
   283→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   284→    if output_file:
   285→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   286→            return
   287→        raise SystemExit(1)
   288→
   289→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   290→    render_findings(
   291→        surfaced_matches,
   292→        pattern=pattern,
   293→        status_filter=status_filter,
   294→        show_code=show_code,
   295→        top=top,
   296→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   297→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   298→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   299→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   300→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   301→    )
   302→    try:
   303→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan as _load_plan
   304→        _plan = _load_plan()
   305→        _plan_active = _plan if (_plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")) else None
   306→    except Exception:
   307→        _plan_active = None
   308→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=_plan_active)
   309→    show_subjective_followup(
   310→        state,
   311→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   312→    )
   313→
   314→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)
   315→
   316→
   317→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
```
