    40→_RISK_SEVERITY_ORDER = {
    41→    "critical": 0,
    42→    "high": 1,
    43→    "medium": 2,
    44→    "low": 3,
    45→    "info": 4,
    46→}
    47→
    48→
    49→DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 95
    50→MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 0
    51→MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 100
    52→_HIGH_IGNORE_SUPPRESSION_THRESHOLD = 30.0
    53→_WONTFIX_GAP_THRESHOLD = 1.0
    54→
    55→
    56→def _resolve_target_strict_score(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str | None]:
    57→    """Resolve strict-score target from config with bounded fallback."""
    58→    raw_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    59→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    60→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE)
    61→    try:
    62→        target = int(raw_target)
    63→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    64→        return (
    65→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    66→            (
    67→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    68→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    69→            ),
    70→        )
    71→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:
    72→        return (
    73→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    74→            (
    75→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    76→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    77→            ),
    78→        )
    79→    return target, None
    80→
    81→
    82→def _compute_strict_target(strict_score: float | None, config: dict | None) -> StrictTarget:
    83→    """Build strict-target context for command rendering and agents."""
    84→    target, warning = _resolve_target_strict_score(config)
    85→    if not isinstance(strict_score, int | float):
    86→        return {
    87→            "target": float(target),
    88→            "current": None,
    89→            "gap": None,
    90→            "state": "unavailable",
    91→            "warning": warning,
    92→        }
    93→
    94→    current = round(float(strict_score), 1)
    95→    gap = round(float(target) - current, 1)
    96→    if gap > 0:
    97→        state = "below"
    98→    elif gap < 0:
    99→        state = "above"
   100→    else:
   101→        state = "at"
   102→    return {
   103→        "target": float(target),
   104→        "current": current,
   105→        "gap": gap,
   106→        "state": state,
   107→        "warning": warning,
   108→    }
   109→
   110→
   111→def _count_open_by_detector(findings: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   112→    """Count open findings by detector, merging structural sub-detectors.
   113→
   114→    When detector is "review" and detail.holistic is True, also increments
   115→    "review_holistic" for separate holistic counting.
   116→    """
   117→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   118→    for f in findings.values():
   119→        if f["status"] != "open" or f.get("suppressed"):
   120→            continue
   121→        detector = f.get("detector", "unknown")
   122→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   123→            detector = "structural"
   124→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   125→        # Track holistic review findings separately
   126→        if detector == "review" and f.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
   127→            by_detector["review_holistic"] = by_detector.get("review_holistic", 0) + 1
   128→    # Track uninvestigated review findings (only when review findings exist)
   129→    if by_detector.get("review", 0) > 0:
   130→        by_detector["review_uninvestigated"] = sum(
   131→            1
   132→            for f in findings.values()
   133→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→            and not f.get("suppressed")
   135→            and f.get("detector") == "review"
   136→            and not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   137→        )
   138→    return by_detector
   139→
   140→
   141→def _resolve_badge_path(project_root: Path) -> tuple[str, Path]:
   142→    """Resolve badge path from config, defaulting to root-level scorecard.png."""
   143→    default_rel = "scorecard.png"
   144→    config = {}
   145→    try:
   146→        config = _load_config()
   147→    except (AttributeError, OSError):
   148→        config = {}
   149→
   150→    raw_path = default_rel
   151→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   152→        configured = config.get("badge_path")
   153→        if isinstance(configured, str) and configured.strip():
   154→            raw_path = configured.strip()
   155→
   156→    path = Path(raw_path)
   157→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   158→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   159→        return raw_path, project_root / path
   160→
   161→    try:
   162→        rel_path = str(path.relative_to(project_root))
   163→    except ValueError:
   164→        rel_path = str(path)
   165→    return rel_path, path
   166→
   167→
   168→def _compute_badge_status() -> BadgeStatus:
   169→    """Check configured scorecard path and whether README references it."""
   170→    project_root = PROJECT_ROOT
   171→
   172→    scorecard_rel, scorecard_path = _resolve_badge_path(project_root)
   173→    generated = scorecard_path.exists()
   174→
   175→    in_readme = False
   176→    if generated:
   177→        for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   178→            readme_path = project_root / readme_name
   179→            if readme_path.exists():
   180→                try:
   181→                    in_readme = scorecard_rel in readme_path.read_text(
   182→                        encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   183→                    )
   184→                except OSError:
   185→                    in_readme = False
   186→                break
   187→
   188→    recommendation = None
   189→    if generated and not in_readme:
   190→        recommendation = (
   191→            f'Add to README: <img src="{scorecard_rel}" width="100%">'
   192→        )
   193→
   194→    return {
   195→        "generated": generated,
   196→        "in_readme": in_readme,
   197→        "path": scorecard_rel,
   198→        "recommendation": recommendation,
   199→    }
   200→
   201→
   202→def _compute_primary_action(actions: list[dict]) -> PrimaryAction | None:
   203→    """Pick the highest-priority action for user-facing guidance."""
   204→    if not actions:
   205→        return None
   206→    top = actions[0]
   207→    command = str(top.get("command", "")).strip()
   208→    if not command:
   209→        return None
   210→    description = str(top.get("description", "")).strip() or "run highest-impact action"
   211→    return {
   212→        "command": command,
   213→        "description": description,
   214→    }
   215→
   216→
   217→def _compute_why_now(
   218→    phase: str,
   219→    strategy: dict[str, object],
   220→    primary_action: dict | None,
   221→) -> str:
   222→    """Explain why the recommended action should happen now."""
   223→    hint = str(strategy.get("hint", "")).strip() if isinstance(strategy, dict) else ""
   224→    if hint:
   225→        return hint
   226→    if primary_action and primary_action.get("description"):
   227→        return str(primary_action["description"])
   228→    phase_default = {
   229→        "first_scan": "Start with highest-impact findings to establish a clean baseline.",
   230→        "regression": "Recent regressions should be contained before new work.",
   231→        "stagnation": "Current approach is stalling; tackle a different high-impact lane.",
   232→        "maintenance": "Keep the codebase stable by resolving new risk quickly.",
   233→    }
   234→    return phase_default.get(phase, "Address the highest-impact open findings first.")
   235→
   236→
   237→def _compute_verification_step(_command: str | None) -> VerificationStep:
   238→    """Verification step returned with every narrative plan."""
   239→    return {
   240→        "command": "desloppify scan",
   241→        "reason": "revalidate after changes",
   242→    }
   243→
   244→
   245→def _compute_risk_flags(state: StateModel, debt: dict) -> list[RiskFlag]:
   246→    """Build ordered risk flags from suppression and wontfix debt signals."""
   247→    flags: list[RiskFlag] = []
   248→
   249→    ignore_integrity = state.get("ignore_integrity", {})
   250→    suppressed_pct = float(ignore_integrity.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   251→    ignored_count = int(ignore_integrity.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   252→    if (
   253→        suppressed_pct >= _HIGH_IGNORE_SUPPRESSION_THRESHOLD
   254→        or ignored_count >= 100
   255→    ):
   256→        severity = "high" if suppressed_pct >= 40.0 or ignored_count >= 200 else "medium"
   257→        message = (
   258→            f"{suppressed_pct:.1f}% findings hidden by ignore patterns"
   259→            if suppressed_pct > 0
   260→            else f"{ignored_count} findings hidden by ignore patterns"
   261→        )
   262→        flags.append(
   263→            {
   264→                "type": "high_ignore_suppression",
   265→                "severity": severity,
   266→                "message": message,
   267→            }
   268→        )
   269→
   270→    wontfix_count = int(debt.get("wontfix_count", 0) or 0)
   271→    overall_gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   272→    if overall_gap >= _WONTFIX_GAP_THRESHOLD or wontfix_count > 0:
   273→        severity = "high" if overall_gap >= 5.0 or wontfix_count >= 50 else "medium"
   274→        flags.append(
   275→            {
   276→                "type": "wontfix_gap",
   277→                "severity": severity,
   278→                "message": (
   279→                    f"Strict/lenient gap is {overall_gap:.1f} pts with "
   280→                    f"{wontfix_count} wontfix findings"
   281→                ),
   282→            }
   283→        )
   284→
   285→    flags.sort(key=lambda flag: _RISK_SEVERITY_ORDER.get(flag.get("severity"), 99))
   286→    return flags
   287→
   288→
   289→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   290→class NarrativeContext:
   291→    """Optional context inputs for narrative computation."""
   292→
   293→    diff: dict | None = None
   294→    lang: str | None = None
   295→    command: str | None = None
   296→    config: dict | None = None
   297→    plan: dict | None = None
   298→
   299→def _history_for_lang(raw_history: list[dict], lang: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   300→    if not lang:
   301→        return raw_history
   302→    return [entry for entry in raw_history if entry.get("lang") in (lang, None)]
   303→
   304→
   305→def _scoped_findings(state: StateModel) -> dict[str, Finding]:
   306→    return path_scoped_findings(
   307→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   308→    )
   309→
   310→
   311→def _score_snapshot(state: StateModel) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   312→    return get_strict_score(state), get_overall_score(state)
   313→
   314→
   315→def compute_narrative(
   316→    state: StateModel,
   317→    context: NarrativeContext | None = None,
   318→) -> NarrativeResult:
   319→    """Compute structured narrative context from state data."""
   320→    resolved_context = context or NarrativeContext()
   321→
   322→    diff = resolved_context.diff
   323→    lang = resolved_context.lang
   324→    command = resolved_context.command
   325→    config = resolved_context.config
   326→    plan = resolved_context.plan
   327→
   328→    raw_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   329→    history = _history_for_lang(raw_history, lang)
   330→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   331→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   332→    strict_score, overall_score = _score_snapshot(state)
   333→    findings = _scoped_findings(state)
   334→
   335→    by_detector = _count_open_by_detector(findings)
   336→    badge = _compute_badge_status()
   337→
   338→    phase = _detect_phase(history, strict_score)
   339→    dimensions = _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores, history, state)
   340→    debt = _analyze_debt(dim_scores, findings, history)
   341→    milestone = _detect_milestone(state, None, history)
   342→    clusters = plan.get("clusters") if isinstance(plan, dict) else None
   343→    action_context = ActionContext(
   344→        by_detector=by_detector,
   345→        dimension_scores=dim_scores,
   346→        state=state,
   347→        debt=debt,
   348→        lang=lang,
   349→        clusters=clusters,
   350→    )
   351→    actions = [dict(action) for action in compute_actions(action_context)]
   352→    strategy = compute_strategy(findings, by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   353→    tools = dict(compute_tools(by_detector, state, lang, badge))
   354→    primary_action = _compute_primary_action(actions)
   355→    why_now = _compute_why_now(phase, strategy, primary_action)
   356→    verification_step = _compute_verification_step(command)
   357→    risk_flags = _compute_risk_flags(state, debt)
   358→    strict_target = _compute_strict_target(strict_score, config)
   359→    headline = _compute_headline(
   360→        phase,
   361→        dimensions,
   362→        debt,
   363→        milestone,
   364→        diff,
   365→        strict_score,
   366→        overall_score,
   367→        stats,
   368→        history,
   369→        open_by_detector=by_detector,
   370→    )
   371→    reminders, updated_reminder_history = _compute_reminders(
   372→        state, lang, phase, debt, actions, dimensions, badge, command, config=config
   373→    )
   374→
   375→    return {
   376→        "phase": phase,
   377→        "headline": headline,
   378→        "dimensions": dimensions,
   379→        "actions": actions,
   380→        "strategy": strategy,
   381→        "tools": tools,
   382→        "debt": debt,
   383→        "milestone": milestone,
   384→        "primary_action": primary_action,
   385→        "why_now": why_now,
   386→        "verification_step": verification_step,
   387→        "risk_flags": risk_flags,
   388→        "strict_target": strict_target,
   389→        "reminders": reminders,
   390→        "reminder_history": updated_reminder_history,
   391→    }
   392→

1→"""Action computation engine used by narrative query generation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ActionContext, ActionItem
    10→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    compute_score_impact,
    13→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    14→    merge_potentials,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    17→
    18→
    19→def supported_fixers(state: StateModel, lang: str | None) -> set[str] | None:
    20→    """Return supported fixers for the active language, or None when unknown."""
    21→    if not lang:
    22→        return None
    23→
    24→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities", {}).get(lang, {})
    25→    fixers = capabilities.get("fixers")
    26→    if isinstance(fixers, list):
    27→        return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
    28→
    29→    try:
    30→        return set(get_lang(lang).fixers.keys())
    31→    except (ImportError, ValueError):
    32→        return None
    33→
    34→
    35→def _impact_calculator(
    36→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    37→    state: StateModel,
    38→) -> Callable[[str, int], float]:
    39→    """Build an impact estimator closure keyed by detector and count."""
    40→    merged_potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    41→    if not merged_potentials or not dimension_scores:
    42→        return lambda _detector, _count: 0.0
    43→
    44→    scoring_view = {
    45→        name: {
    46→            "score": values["score"],
    47→            "tier": values.get("tier", 3),
    48→            "detectors": values.get("detectors", {}),
    49→        }
    50→        for name, values in dimension_scores.items()
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    def _impact(detector: str, count: int) -> float:
    54→        return compute_score_impact(
    55→            scoring_view, merged_potentials, detector, count
    56→        )
    57→
    58→    return _impact
    59→
    60→
    61→def _dimension_name(detector: str) -> str:
    62→    """Resolve user-facing dimension name for a detector."""
    63→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    64→    return dimension.name if dimension else "Unknown"
    65→
    66→
    67→def _fixer_has_applicable_findings(
    68→    state: StateModel, detector: str, fixer_name: str
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    """For the smells detector, verify the fixer has matching open findings.
    71→
    72→    The smells detector aggregates many smell types but each fixer only handles
    73→    one sub-type (e.g. ``dead-useeffect`` only fixes ``dead_useeffect`` smells).
    74→    Without this check, a React-specific fixer can be suggested to projects that
    75→    have no React code at all, producing a confusing "Found 0 candidates" result.
    76→
    77→    For all other detectors the fixer is considered universally applicable.
    78→    """
    79→    if detector != "smells":
    80→        return True
    81→    smell_id = fixer_name.replace("-", "_")
    82→    return any(
    83→        f.get("status") == "open"
    84→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    85→        and f.get("detector") == "smells"
    86→        and f.get("detail", {}).get("smell_id") == smell_id
    87→        for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _append_auto_fix_actions(
    92→    actions: list[ActionItem],
    93→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    94→    supported: set[str] | None,
    95→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    96→    state: StateModel,
    97→) -> None:
    98→    """Append auto-fix/manual-fix actions for detectors with auto fixers."""
    99→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   100→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
   101→            continue
   102→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   103→        if count == 0:
   104→            continue
   105→
   106→        impact = round(impact_for(detector, count), 1)
   107→        available_fixers = [
   108→            fixer
   109→            for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]
   110→            if (supported is None or fixer in supported)
   111→            and _fixer_has_applicable_findings(state, detector, fixer)
   112→        ]
   113→        if not available_fixers:
   114→            actions.append(
   115→                {
   116→                    "type": "manual_fix",
   117→                    "detector": detector,
   118→                    "count": count,
   119→                    "description": (
   120→                        f"{count} {detector} findings — inspect with "
   121→                        f"`desloppify next` and fix manually"
   122→                    ),
   123→                    "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   124→                    "impact": impact,
   125→                    "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   126→                }
   127→            )
   128→            continue
   129→
   130→        fixer = available_fixers[0]
   131→        actions.append(
   132→            {
   133→                "type": "auto_fix",
   134→                "detector": detector,
   135→                "count": count,
   136→                "description": (
   137→                    f"{count} {detector} findings — run "
   138→                    f"`desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run` to preview, then apply"
   139→                ),
   140→                "command": f"desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run",
   141→                "impact": impact,
   142→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   143→            }
   144→        )
   145→
   146→
   147→def _append_reorganize_actions(
   148→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   149→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   150→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   151→) -> None:
   152→    """Append structure/move oriented actions."""
   153→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   154→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "reorganize":
   155→            continue
   156→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   157→        if count == 0:
   158→            continue
   159→
   160→        guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "restructure with move")
   161→        actions.append(
   162→            {
   163→                "type": "reorganize",
   164→                "detector": detector,
   165→                "count": count,
   166→                "description": f"{count} {detector} findings — {guidance}",
   167→                "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   168→                "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   169→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   170→            }
   171→        )
   172→
   173→
   174→def _build_refactor_entry(
   175→    detector: str,
   176→    tool_info: dict[str, Any],
   177→    count: int,
   178→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   179→) -> ActionItem:
   180→    """Build one refactor/manual action row."""
   181→    guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "manual fix")
   182→    adjusted_info = {**tool_info, "guidance": guidance}
   183→
   184→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   185→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   186→        description = f"{count} files need design review — run holistic review to refresh subjective scores"
   187→    elif detector == "review":
   188→        command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   189→        suffix = "s" if count != 1 else ""
   190→        description = (
   191→            f"{count} review finding{suffix} need investigation — "
   192→            "run `desloppify show review --status open` to see them"
   193→        )
   194→        adjusted_info = {**adjusted_info, "action_type": "refactor"}
   195→    else:
   196→        command = f"desloppify show {detector} --status open"
   197→        description = f"{count} {detector} findings — {guidance}"
   198→
   199→    return {
   200→        "type": adjusted_info["action_type"],
   201→        "detector": detector,
   202→        "count": count,
   203→        "description": description,
   204→        "command": command,
   205→        "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   206→        "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def _append_refactor_actions(
   211→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   212→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   213→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   214→) -> None:
   215→    """Append refactor/manual actions after auto-fix/reorg buckets."""
   216→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   217→        if tool_info["action_type"] not in {"refactor", "manual_fix"}:
   218→            continue
   219→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   220→        if count == 0:
   221→            continue
   222→        actions.append(_build_refactor_entry(detector, tool_info, count, impact_for))
   223→
   224→
   225→def _append_debt_action(actions: list[ActionItem], debt: dict[str, float]) -> None:
   226→    """Append wontfix-debt callout when gap is material."""
   227→    gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   228→    if gap <= 2.0:
   229→        return
   230→    actions.append(
   231→        {
   232→            "type": "debt_review",
   233→            "detector": None,
   234→            "description": f"{gap} pts of wontfix debt — review stale decisions",
   235→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   236→            "gap": gap,
   237→        }
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→def _assign_priorities(actions: list[ActionItem]) -> list[ActionItem]:
   242→    """Sort and assign sequential priorities."""
   243→    type_order = {
   244→        "issue_queue": 0,
   245→        "auto_fix": 1,
   246→        "reorganize": 2,
   247→        "refactor": 3,
   248→        "manual_fix": 4,
   249→        "debt_review": 5,
   250→    }
   251→    actions.sort(
   252→        key=lambda action: (type_order.get(action["type"], 9), -action.get("impact", 0))
   253→    )
   254→    for index, action in enumerate(actions, start=1):
   255→        action["priority"] = index
   256→    return actions
   257→
   258→
   259→def _cluster_detector(cluster: dict) -> str | None:
   260→    """Extract the primary detector from a cluster.
   261→
   262→    Uses the cluster_key prefix (e.g. "auto::unused" → "unused",
   263→    "typed::dict_keys::phantom_read" → "dict_keys") or falls back
   264→    to the first member's detector.
   265→    """
   266→    key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   267→    if key:
   268→        parts = key.split("::")
   269→        if len(parts) >= 2:
   270→            return parts[1]
   271→    # Fallback: parse from cluster name (auto/detector-subtype)
   272→    name = cluster.get("name", "")
   273→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   274→        rest = name[5:]
   275→        return rest.split("-", 1)[0] if "-" in rest else rest
   276→    return None
   277→
   278→
   279→def _annotate_with_clusters(
   280→    actions: list[ActionItem], clusters: dict | None
   281→) -> None:
   282→    """Annotate actions with matching cluster info when clusters exist."""
   283→    if not clusters:
   284→        return
   285→    for action in actions:
   286→        detector = action.get("detector")
   287→        if not detector:
   288→            continue
   289→        matching = [
   290→            name
   291→            for name, c in clusters.items()
   292→            if c.get("auto") and _cluster_detector(c) == detector
   293→        ]
   294→        if matching:
   295→            action["clusters"] = matching
   296→            action["cluster_count"] = len(matching)
   297→            action["command"] = "desloppify next"
   298→            count = action.get("count", 0)
   299→            display = action.get("detector", "unknown")
   300→            action["description"] = (
   301→                f"{count} {display} findings in {len(matching)} cluster(s) — "
   302→                f"run `desloppify next`"
   303→            )
   304→
   305→
   306→def compute_actions(ctx: ActionContext) -> list[ActionItem]:
   307→    """Compute prioritized action list with tool mapping."""
   308→    actions: list[ActionItem] = []
   309→    impact_for = _impact_calculator(ctx.dimension_scores, ctx.state)
   310→    supported = supported_fixers(ctx.state, ctx.lang)
   311→
   312→    _append_auto_fix_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, supported, impact_for, ctx.state)
   313→    _append_reorganize_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
   314→    _append_refactor_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
   315→    _append_debt_action(actions, ctx.debt)
   316→
   317→    prioritized = _assign_priorities(actions)
   318→    _annotate_with_clusters(prioritized, ctx.clusters)
   319→    return prioritized
   320→

1→"""Execution strategy engine for narrative lanes and parallelization hints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Iterable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import (
     9→    _DETECTOR_CASCADE,
    10→    STRUCTURAL_MERGE,
    11→)
    12→
    13→
    14→def open_files_by_detector(findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
    15→    """Collect file sets of open findings by detector."""
    16→    by_detector: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    17→    for finding in findings.values():
    18→        if finding["status"] != "open" or finding.get("suppressed"):
    19→            continue
    20→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    21→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    22→            detector = "structural"
    23→        file_path = finding.get("file", "")
    24→        if not file_path:
    25→            by_detector.setdefault(detector, set())
    26→            continue
    27→        by_detector.setdefault(detector, set()).add(file_path)
    28→    return by_detector
    29→
    30→
    31→def compute_fixer_leverage(
    32→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    33→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
    34→    phase: str,
    35→    _lang: str | None,
    36→) -> dict[str, float | int | str]:
    37→    """Estimate how much value automated fixers would deliver."""
    38→    auto_fixable = sum(
    39→        action.get("count", 0) for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    40→    )
    41→    total = sum(by_detector.values())
    42→    coverage = auto_fixable / total if total > 0 else 0.0
    43→    total_impact = sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in actions)
    44→    auto_impact = sum(
    45→        action.get("impact", 0)
    46→        for action in actions
    47→        if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    48→    )
    49→    impact_ratio = auto_impact / total_impact if total_impact > 0 else 0.0
    50→
    51→    if coverage == 0:
    52→        recommendation = "none"
    53→    elif coverage > 0.4 or impact_ratio > 0.3:
    54→        recommendation = "strong"
    55→    elif phase in ("first_scan", "stagnation", "regression") and coverage > 0.15:
    56→        recommendation = "strong"
    57→    elif coverage > 0.1:
    58→        recommendation = "moderate"
    59→    else:
    60→        recommendation = "none"
    61→
    62→    return {
    63→        "auto_fixable_count": auto_fixable,
    64→        "total_count": total,
    65→        "coverage": round(coverage, 3),
    66→        "impact_ratio": round(impact_ratio, 3),
    67→        "recommendation": recommendation,
    68→    }
    69→
    70→
    71→def _cleanup_lane_actions(actions: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    72→    cascade_rank = {detector: idx for idx, detector in enumerate(_DETECTOR_CASCADE)}
    73→
    74→    def sort_key(action: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, float]:
    75→        detector = action.get("detector", "")
    76→        return (cascade_rank.get(detector, 99), -action.get("impact", 0))
    77→
    78→    return sorted(actions, key=sort_key)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _files_for_actions(
    82→    actions: Iterable[dict[str, Any]], files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]]
    83→) -> set[str]:
    84→    files: set[str] = set()
    85→    for action in actions:
    86→        detector = action.get("detector")
    87→        if detector and detector in files_by_detector:
    88→            files |= files_by_detector[detector]
    89→    return files
    90→
    91→
    92→def _group_by_file_overlap(
    93→    action_files: list[tuple[dict[str, Any], set[str]]],
    94→) -> list[list[tuple[dict[str, Any], set[str]]]]:
    95→    """Group actions whose file sets overlap using union-find."""
    96→    item_count = len(action_files)
    97→    if item_count == 0:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    parent = list(range(item_count))
   101→
   102→    def find(index: int) -> int:
   103→        while parent[index] != index:
   104→            parent[index] = parent[parent[index]]
   105→            index = parent[index]
   106→        return index
   107→
   108→    def union(left: int, right: int) -> None:
   109→        left_root, right_root = find(left), find(right)
   110→        if left_root != right_root:
   111→            parent[left_root] = right_root
   112→
   113→    for left in range(item_count):
   114→        for right in range(left + 1, item_count):
   115→            if action_files[left][1] & action_files[right][1]:
   116→                union(left, right)
   117→
   118→    grouped_indices: dict[int, list[int]] = {}
   119→    for index in range(item_count):
   120→        grouped_indices.setdefault(find(index), []).append(index)
   121→
   122→    return [
   123→        [action_files[index] for index in indices]
   124→        for indices in grouped_indices.values()
   125→    ]
   126→
   127→
   128→def _refactor_lanes(
   129→    refactor_actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   130→    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
   131→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   132→    """Split refactor work into independent lanes by file overlap."""
   133→    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
   134→    action_files = [
   135→        (action, files_by_detector.get(action.get("detector"), set()))
   136→        for action in refactor_actions
   137→    ]
   138→
   139→    test_coverage_actions = [
   140→        (action, files)
   141→        for action, files in action_files
   142→        if action.get("detector") == "test_coverage"
   143→    ]
   144→    other_actions = [
   145→        (action, files)
   146→        for action, files in action_files
   147→        if action.get("detector") != "test_coverage"
   148→    ]
   149→
   150→    groups = _group_by_file_overlap(other_actions)
   151→    for index, group in enumerate(groups):
   152→        lane_name = f"refactor_{index}" if len(groups) > 1 else "refactor"
   153→        lanes[lane_name] = {
   154→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action, _ in group],
   155→            "file_count": len(
   156→                _files_for_actions((action for action, _ in group), files_by_detector)
   157→            ),
   158→            "total_impact": round(
   159→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action, _ in group), 1
   160→            ),
   161→            "automation": "manual",
   162→            "run_first": False,
   163→        }
   164→
   165→    if test_coverage_actions:
   166→        lanes["test_coverage"] = {
   167→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action, _ in test_coverage_actions],
   168→            "file_count": len(
   169→                _files_for_actions(
   170→                    (action for action, _ in test_coverage_actions), files_by_detector
   171→                )
   172→            ),
   173→            "total_impact": round(
   174→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action, _ in test_coverage_actions), 1
   175→            ),
   176→            "automation": "manual",
   177→            "run_first": False,
   178→        }
   179→
   180→    return lanes
   181→
   182→
   183→def compute_lanes(
   184→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   185→    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
   186→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   187→    """Partition actions into parallelizable work lanes."""
   188→    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
   189→
   190→    cleanup_actions = _cleanup_lane_actions(
   191→        [action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"]
   192→    )
   193→    reorganize_actions = [
   194→        action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "reorganize"
   195→    ]
   196→    debt_actions = [action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "debt_review"]
   197→    refactor_actions = [
   198→        action
   199→        for action in actions
   200→        if action.get("type") not in {"auto_fix", "reorganize", "debt_review"}
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    if cleanup_actions:
   204→        cleanup_files = _files_for_actions(cleanup_actions, files_by_detector)
   205→        lanes["cleanup"] = {
   206→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in cleanup_actions],
   207→            "file_count": len(cleanup_files),
   208→            "total_impact": round(
   209→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in cleanup_actions), 1
   210→            ),
   211→            "automation": "full",
   212→            "run_first": False,
   213→        }
   214→
   215→    if reorganize_actions:
   216→        lanes["restructure"] = {
   217→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in reorganize_actions],
   218→            "file_count": len(
   219→                _files_for_actions(reorganize_actions, files_by_detector)
   220→            ),
   221→            "total_impact": round(
   222→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in reorganize_actions), 1
   223→            ),
   224→            "automation": "manual",
   225→            "run_first": False,
   226→        }
   227→
   228→    if refactor_actions:
   229→        lanes.update(_refactor_lanes(refactor_actions, files_by_detector))
   230→
   231→    if debt_actions:
   232→        lanes["debt_review"] = {
   233→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in debt_actions],
   234→            "file_count": 0,
   235→            "total_impact": 0.0,
   236→            "automation": "manual",
   237→            "run_first": False,
   238→        }
   239→
   240→    if "cleanup" in lanes:
   241→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items():
   242→            if lane_name == "cleanup":
   243→                continue
   244→            lane_files = _files_for_actions(
   245→                (action for action in actions if action["priority"] in lane["actions"]),
   246→                files_by_detector,
   247→            )
   248→            if cleanup_files & lane_files:
   249→                lanes["cleanup"]["run_first"] = True
   250→                break
   251→
   252→    return lanes
   253→
   254→
   255→def _significant_lane(lane_name: str, lane: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   256→    if lane_name == "debt_review" or lane.get("run_first"):
   257→        return False
   258→    return lane.get("file_count", 0) >= 5 or lane.get("total_impact", 0) >= 1.0
   259→
   260→
   261→def compute_strategy_hint(
   262→    fixer_leverage: dict[str, Any],
   263→    lanes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   264→    can_parallelize: bool,
   265→    phase: str,
   266→) -> str:
   267→    """Generate one- or two-sentence execution strategy guidance."""
   268→    recommendation = fixer_leverage.get("recommendation", "none")
   269→    coverage_pct = round(fixer_leverage.get("coverage", 0) * 100)
   270→    lane_count = sum(
   271→        1
   272→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items()
   273→        if lane_name != "debt_review" and not lane.get("run_first")
   274→    )
   275→
   276→    if recommendation == "strong" and can_parallelize:
   277→        return (
   278→            f"Run fixers first — they cover {coverage_pct}% of findings. "
   279→            f"Then {lane_count} independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. "
   280→            "Rescan after each phase to verify."
   281→        )
   282→    if recommendation == "strong":
   283→        return (
   284→            f"Run fixers first — they cover {coverage_pct}% of findings. "
   285→            "Then rescan to verify."
   286→        )
   287→    if can_parallelize:
   288→        return (
   289→            f"{lane_count} independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. "
   290→            "Rescan after each phase to verify."
   291→        )
   292→    if phase == "maintenance":
   293→        return "Maintenance mode — address new findings as they appear."
   294→    if phase == "stagnation":
   295→        return "Try a different dimension to break the plateau."
   296→    return "Work through actions in priority order. Rescan after each fix to track progress."
   297→
   298→
   299→def compute_strategy(
   300→    findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   301→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   302→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   303→    phase: str,
   304→    lang: str | None,
   305→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   306→    """Orchestrate strategy computation and annotate actions with lanes."""
   307→    files_by_detector = open_files_by_detector(findings)
   308→    fixer_leverage = compute_fixer_leverage(by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   309→    lanes = compute_lanes(actions, files_by_detector)
   310→
   311→    action_lane: dict[int, str] = {}
   312→    for lane_name, lane in lanes.items():
   313→        for priority in lane["actions"]:
   314→            action_lane[priority] = lane_name
   315→    for action in actions:
   316→        action["lane"] = action_lane.get(action["priority"])
   317→
   318→    significant_non_blocked = [
   319→        (lane_name, lane)
   320→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items()
   321→        if _significant_lane(lane_name, lane)
   322→    ]
   323→    can_parallelize = len(significant_non_blocked) >= 2
   324→
   325→    hint = compute_strategy_hint(fixer_leverage, lanes, can_parallelize, phase)
   326→    review_action = next(
   327→        (action for action in actions if action.get("detector") == "review"), None
   328→    )
   329→    if review_action:
   330→        hint += f" Review: {review_action['count']} finding(s) — `desloppify show review --status open`."
   331→
   332→    return {
   333→        "fixer_leverage": fixer_leverage,
   334→        "lanes": {name: {**lane} for name, lane in lanes.items()},
   335→        "can_parallelize": can_parallelize,
   336→        "hint": hint,
   337→    }
   338→

     5→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_subjective import (
     7→    build_subjective_followup,
     8→)
     9→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    10→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE, group_queue_items
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    subjective_review_open_breakdown,
    16→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    19→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    20→
    21→
    22→def scorecard_subjective(
    23→    state: dict,
    24→    dim_scores: dict,
    25→) -> list[dict]:
    26→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    27→    if not dim_scores:
    28→        return []
    29→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    30→        state,
    31→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    32→    )
    33→
    34→
    35→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    36→    findings_scoped: dict,
    37→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    38→    """Return open subjective-review count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    39→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(findings_scoped)
    40→
    41→
    42→def _tier_label(tier: int) -> str:
    43→    return f"T{tier}"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _render_tier_navigator(queue: dict) -> None:
    47→    counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    48→    print(colorize("\n  Tier Navigator", "bold"))
    49→    print(
    50→        colorize(
    51→            f"    Open: T1:{counts.get(1, 0)} T2:{counts.get(2, 0)} "
    52→            f"T3:{counts.get(3, 0)} T4:{counts.get(4, 0)}",
    53→            "dim",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    print(
    57→        colorize(
    58→            "    Switch: `desloppify next --tier 1` | `desloppify next --tier 2` | "
    59→            "`desloppify next --tier 3` | `desloppify next --tier 4`",
    60→            "dim",
    61→        )
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    68→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    69→        for item in grouped_items:
    70→            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    71→            tag = _effort_tag(item)
    72→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    73→            print(
    74→                f"    {_tier_label(tier)} [{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    75→            )
    76→
    77→
    78→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    79→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    80→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    81→
    82→
    83→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    84→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    85→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    86→        return "[review]"
    87→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    88→        return "[auto]"
    89→    return ""
    90→
    91→
    92→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    93→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",
    94→    "reorganize": "Reorganize batch",
    95→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    96→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",
    97→}
    98→
    99→
   100→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   101→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   102→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   103→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   104→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   105→    if cluster_name == "auto/initial-review":
   106→        type_label = "Initial subjective review"
   107→    elif cluster_name == "auto/stale-review":
   108→        type_label = "Stale subjective review"
   109→    else:
   110→        type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
   111→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
   112→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   113→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   114→
   115→    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
   116→    members = item.get("members", [])
   117→    if members:
   118→        # File distribution
   119→        from collections import Counter
   120→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   121→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   122→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   123→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   124→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   125→        else:
   126→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   127→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   128→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   129→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   130→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   131→
   132→        # Sample IDs
   133→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   134→        for m in members[:3]:
   135→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   136→        if len(members) > 3:
   137→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   138→
   139→    # Action
   140→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   141→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   142→    if primary_command:
   143→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   144→
   145→    # Resolution hints
   146→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" '
   147→                   f'--note "<what>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"', "dim"))
   148→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   149→                   "dim"))
   150→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   151→
   152→
   153→def _render_item(
   154→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   155→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   158→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   159→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   163→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   164→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   165→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   166→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   167→
   168→    # Effort/type label
   169→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   170→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   171→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   172→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   173→
   174→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   175→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   176→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   177→        subjective_score = float(
   178→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   179→        )
   180→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   181→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")
   182→        print(
   183→            colorize(
   184→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   185→                "cyan",
   186→            )
   187→        )
   188→        print(colorize(
   189→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   190→            "dim",
   191→        ))
   192→        if explain:
   193→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   194→                "policy",
   195→                "subjective items are fixed at T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3.",
   196→            )
   197→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   198→        return
   199→
   200→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   201→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   202→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   203→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   204→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   205→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   206→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   207→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   208→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   209→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   210→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   211→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   212→
   213→    print(f"  File: {item.get('file', '')}")
   214→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   215→
   216→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   217→    if detail.get("lines"):
   218→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   219→    if detail.get("category"):
   220→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   221→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   222→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   223→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   224→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   225→
   226→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   227→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   228→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   229→        if snippet:
   230→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   231→            print(snippet)
   232→
   233→    if dim_scores:
   234→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   235→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   236→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   237→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   238→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   239→            print(
   240→                colorize(
   241→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   242→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   243→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   244→                    "dim",
   245→                )
   246→            )
   247→
   248→    # Score impact estimate
   249→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   250→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   251→        try:
   252→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_score_impact
   253→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   254→            if impact > 0:
   255→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   256→            else:
   257→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   258→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   259→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   260→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   261→                    if issues > 1:
   262→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   263→                        if bulk > 0:
   264→                            print(colorize(
   265→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   266→                                "cyan",
   267→                            ))
   268→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   269→            pass
   270→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   271→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   272→        try:
   273→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
   274→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   275→            if dim_key:
   276→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   277→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   278→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   279→                        continue
   280→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   281→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   282→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   283→                        if drag > 0.01:
   284→                            print(colorize(
   285→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   286→                                "cyan",
   287→                            ))
   288→                        break
   289→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   290→            pass
   291→
   292→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   293→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   294→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   295→        similar_count = sum(
   296→            1
   297→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   298→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   299→        )
   300→        if similar_count > 1:
   301→            print(
   302→                colorize(
   303→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   304→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   305→                    "cyan",
   306→                )
   307→            )
   308→    if explain:
   309→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   310→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   311→        base = (
   312→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   313→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   314→        )
   315→
   316→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   317→        if dim_scores and detector:
   318→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   319→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   320→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   321→                base += (
   322→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   323→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   324→                )
   325→
   326→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   327→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   328→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   329→            if dim_key:
   330→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   331→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   332→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   333→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   334→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   335→                        break
   336→
   337→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   338→        if policy:
   339→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   340→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   341→
   342→
   343→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   344→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   345→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   346→        return
   347→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   348→    if not explain:
   349→        return
   350→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   351→    if available:
   352→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   353→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   354→
   355→
   356→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   357→    if queue.get("items"):
   358→        return False
   359→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   360→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   361→    if tier is not None:
   362→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   363→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   364→        if available:
   365→            commands = " | ".join(
   366→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   367→            )
   368→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   369→    return True
   370→
   371→
   372→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   373→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   374→    if not plan:
   375→        return
   376→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   377→    summary = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   378→    if not summary:
   379→        return
   380→    print(colorize("\n  Meta-plan:", "bold"))
   381→    for line in summary.strip().splitlines():
   382→        print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
   383→
   384→
   385→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   386→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   387→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   388→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   389→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   390→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   391→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   392→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   393→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   394→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   395→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   396→
   397→    print(colorize("  (Meta-plan update needed, T1)", "bold"))
   398→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   399→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   400→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   401→    print()
   402→    if new:
   403→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   404→    if resolved:
   405→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   406→    if epic_count:
   407→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   408→    print()
   409→    print("  Action:   desloppify plan synthesize")
   410→    print("  Preview:  desloppify plan synthesize --dry-run")
   411→    print()
   412→    print("  View plan:")
   413→    print("    desloppify plan queue              — see all items in priority order")
   414→    print("    desloppify plan show               — plan status summary")
   415→
   416→
   417→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   418→    """Render an epic card."""
   419→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   420→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   421→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   422→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   423→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   424→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   425→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   426→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   427→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   428→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   429→
   430→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   431→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   432→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   433→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   434→    if root_cause:
   435→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   436→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   437→    if total_epics:
   438→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   439→    print()
   440→    if action_steps:
   441→        print("  Steps:")
   442→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   443→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   444→        print()
   445→    if finding_ids:
   446→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   447→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   448→        for fid in shown:
   449→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   450→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   451→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   452→        print()
   453→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   454→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   455→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   456→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   457→
   458→
   459→def render_terminal_items(
   460→    items: list[dict],
   461→    dim_scores: dict,
   462→    findings_scoped: dict,
   463→    *,
   464→    group: str,
   465→    explain: bool,
   466→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   467→    plan: dict | None = None,
   468→) -> None:
   469→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   470→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   471→
   472→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   473→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   474→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   475→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   476→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   477→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   478→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   479→
   480→    if group != "item":
   481→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   482→        return
   483→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   484→        if idx > 0:
   485→            print()
   486→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   487→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   488→
   489→        # Special rendering for synthesis and epic items
   490→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   491→            _render_synthesis_item(item)
   492→            continue
   493→        if kind == "epic":
   494→            _render_epic_item(item)
   495→            continue
   496→
   497→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   498→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   499→        elif len(items) > 1:
   500→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   501→        else:
   502→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   503→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   504→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   505→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   506→
   507→
   508→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   509→    if len(items) != 1:
   510→        return
   511→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   512→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   513→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   514→    if kind != "finding":
   515→        return
   516→    item = items[0]
   517→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   518→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   519→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   520→        primary = item.get(
   521→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   522→        )
   523→        print(f"    {primary}")
   524→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   525→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   526→        return
   527→
   528→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   529→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   530→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   531→        print(f"    {primary}")
   532→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   533→    else:
   534→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   535→
   536→    print(
   537→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   538→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   539→    )
   540→    print(
   541→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   542→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   543→    )
   544→
   545→
   546→def render_followup_nudges(
   547→    state: dict,
   548→    dim_scores: dict,
   549→    findings_scoped: dict,
   550→    *,
   551→    strict_score: float | None,
   552→    target_strict_score: float,
   553→) -> None:
   554→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   555→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   556→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   557→        state,
   558→        subjective_entries,
   559→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   560→        max_quality_items=3,
   561→        max_integrity_items=5,
   562→    )
   563→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   564→    if strict_score is not None:
   565→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   566→        if gap > 0:
   567→            print(
   568→                colorize(
   569→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   570→                    "cyan",
   571→                )
   572→            )
   573→        else:
   574→            print(
   575→                colorize(
   576→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   577→                    "green",
   578→                )
   579→            )
   580→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   581→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   582→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   583→
   584→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   585→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   586→        findings_scoped
   587→    )
   588→    parts: list[str] = []
   589→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   590→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   591→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   592→    open_review = [
   593→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   594→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   595→    ]
   596→    if low_dims:
   597→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   598→    if stale_count:
   599→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   600→    if unassessed_count:
   601→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   602→    if len(open_review):
   603→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   604→    if coverage_open > 0:
   605→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   606→
   607→    if parts:
   608→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   609→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   610→
   611→
   612→__all__ = [
   613→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   614→    "render_followup_nudges",
   615→    "render_queue_header",
   616→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   617→    "render_terminal_items",
   618→    "scorecard_subjective",
   619→    "show_empty_queue",
   620→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   621→]
   622→

    37→def _print_score_lines(

    56→    print("Score guide:")
    57→    print("  overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)")
    58→    print("  objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)")
    59→    print("  strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star")
    60→    print("  verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes")

    64→def _split_dimension_scores(

   123→def _print_drag_summary(dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   124→    """Print the biggest score-drag dimensions so agents know where to focus."""
   125→    if not dim_scores:

   132→            key=lambda e: -float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0),
   133→        )
   134→        if drags:

   141→                )

   220→def _try_auto_update_skill() -> None:

   226→    install = skill_docs_mod.find_installed_skill()

   237→        if install:

   242→        if interface:

   248→def _print_badge_hint(badge_path: Path | None) -> None:

   251→
   252→    rel_path = badge_path.name if badge_path.parent == PROJECT_ROOT else str(badge_path)
   253→    print(f"A scorecard image was saved to `{rel_path}`.")

   255→    print(f'to their README: `<img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">`')
   256→
   257→
   258→def _print_llm_summary(
   259→    state: dict,
   260→    badge_path: Path | None,
   261→    narrative: dict | None = None,
   262→    diff: dict | None = None,
   263→):
   264→    """Print a structured summary for LLM consumption.
   265→
   266→    The LLM reads terminal output after running scans. This gives it
   267→    clear instructions on how to present the results to the end user.
   268→    Only shown when running inside an agent (CLAUDE_CODE or DESLOPPIFY_AGENT env).
   269→    """
   270→    if not _is_agent_environment():
   271→        return
   272→
   273→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   274→    scores = _load_scores(state)
   275→
   276→    if (
   277→        scores.overall is None
   278→        and scores.objective is None
   279→        and scores.strict is None
   280→        and scores.verified is None
   281→        and not dim_scores
   282→    ):
   283→        return
   284→
   285→    print("─" * 60)
   286→    print("INSTRUCTIONS FOR LLM")
   287→    print("IMPORTANT: ALWAYS present ALL scores to the user after a scan.")
   288→    print("Show overall health (lenient + strict), ALL dimension scores,")
   289→    print("AND all subjective dimension scores in a markdown table.")
   290→    print("The goal is to maximize strict scores. Never skip the scores.\n")
   291→
   292→    # Check for living plan
   293→    try:
   294→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   295→        _plan = load_plan()
   296→        _has_plan = bool(
   297→            _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   298→            or _plan.get("skipped")
   299→        )
   300→    except Exception:
   301→        _plan = {}
   302→        _has_plan = False
   303→
   304→    if _has_plan:
   305→        ordered = len(_plan.get("queue_order", []))
   306→        skipped = len(_plan.get("skipped", {}))
   307→        active = _plan.get("active_cluster")
   308→        print(f"LIVING PLAN ACTIVE: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.")
   309→        if active:
   310→            cluster = _plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   311→            remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   312→            print(f"Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).")
   313→        print("The plan is the single source of truth for work order.")
   314→        print("Use `desloppify next` which respects the plan.")
   315→        print("Use `desloppify plan` to view and update it.\n")
   316→
   317→    _print_score_lines(
   318→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   319→        objective_score=scores.objective,
   320→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   321→        verified_score=scores.verified,
   322→    )
   323→    _print_dimension_table(state, dim_scores)
   324→    _print_drag_summary(dim_scores)
   325→    _print_stats_summary(
   326→        state,
   327→        diff,
   328→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   329→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   330→    )
   331→    if _has_plan:
   332→        print("\nFollow the living plan: `desloppify next` for your next task,")
   333→        print("`desloppify plan` to view the full queue.")
   334→    else:
   335→        _print_workflow_guide()
   336→    _print_narrative_status(narrative)
   337→    _print_badge_hint(badge_path)
   338→    print("─" * 60)
   339→
   340→
   341→def auto_update_skill() -> None:
   342→    """Auto-install or update the skill document if we detect an agent.
   343→
   344→    Called unconditionally from the scan workflow — not gated on scores.
   345→    """
   346→    if not _is_agent_environment():
   347→        return
   348→
   349→    _try_auto_update_skill()
   350→
   351→    # Single post-check: whatever happened above, is the doc current now?
   352→    install = skill_docs_mod.find_installed_skill()
   353→    if not install:
   354→        names = ", ".join(sorted(skill_docs_mod.SKILL_TARGETS))
   355→        print(
   356→            f"No skill document found. Install one for better workflow guidance: "
   357→            f"desloppify update-skill <{names}>"
   358→        )
   359→    elif install.stale:
   360→        print(
   361→            f"Skill document is outdated "
   362→            f"(v{install.version}, current v{skill_docs_mod.SKILL_VERSION}). "
   363→            f"Run: desloppify update-skill"
   364→        )

   367→__all__ = ["_print_llm_summary", "auto_update_skill"]

1→"""Query output helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.query_paths import query_file_path
    10→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    11→from desloppify.core.query import write_query as _core_write_query
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueryWriter:
    17→    """Command-level query artifact writer with explicit failure policy."""
    18→
    19→    query_file: Path
    20→
    21→    def write(self, data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    22→        return _core_write_query(data, query_file=self.query_file)
    23→
    24→    def write_best_effort(self, data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    25→        result = self.write(data)
    26→        if not result.ok:
    27→            detail = result.message or "unknown write failure"
    28→            print(
    29→                colorize(
    30→                    f"  Warning: {context} succeeded but query artifact was not written ({detail}).",
    31→                    "yellow",
    32→                ),
    33→                file=sys.stderr,
    34→            )
    35→        return result
    36→
    37→
    38→def query_writer() -> QueryWriter:
    39→    """Construct a writer bound to the active runtime query path."""
    40→    return QueryWriter(query_file=query_file_path())
    41→
    42→
    43→def write_query(data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    44→    """Write structured query output using default best-effort policy."""
    45→    return write_query_best_effort(data, context="query payload update")
    46→
    47→
    48→def write_query_best_effort(data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    49→    """Write query payload and emit a contextual warning on failure."""
    50→    return query_writer().write_best_effort(data, context=context)
    51→
    52→
    53→__all__ = [
    54→    "QueryWriter",
    55→    "query_file_path",
    56→    "query_writer",
    57→    "write_query",
    58→    "write_query_best_effort",
    59→]
    60→
