```
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_agent_plan(
    12→    steps: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    next_command: str | None = None,
    15→    header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:",
    16→    plan: dict | None = None,
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print a consistent AGENT PLAN block with numbered steps.
    19→
    20→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead
    21→    of the narrative-derived steps.
    22→    """
    23→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
    24→        _print_plan_agent_block(plan, header=header)
    25→        return
    26→    if not steps:
    27→        return
    28→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    29→    for idx, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {idx}. {step}", "dim"))
    31→    if next_command:
    32→        print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _print_plan_agent_block(plan: dict, *, header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:") -> None:
    36→    """Render the living plan as the agent plan block."""
    37→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    38→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    39→    skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    42→    print(colorize(f"  Living plan active: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.", "dim"))
    43→    if active:
    44→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    45→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    46→        print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).", "dim"))
    47→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    48→    print(colorize("  View plan: `desloppify plan`", "dim"))
    49→
    50→
    51→def print_replacement_groups(
    52→    groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],
    53→    *,
    54→    title: str,
    55→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] = rel,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    """Print grouped old→new replacement lines by file."""
    58→    if not groups:
    59→        return
    60→    print(colorize(title, "cyan"))
    61→    for filepath, replacements in sorted(groups.items()):
    62→        print(f"    {rel_fn(filepath)}:")
    63→        for old, new in replacements:
    64→            print(f"      {old}  →  {new}")
    65→    print()
    66→
    67→
    68→def print_ranked_actions(
    69→    actions: list[dict], *, limit: int = 3, plan: dict | None = None
    70→) -> bool:
    71→    """Print the highest-impact narrative actions and return True when shown."""
    72→    ranked = sorted(
    73→        [action for action in actions if int(action.get("count", 0)) > 0],
    74→        key=lambda action: (
    75→            -float(action.get("impact", 0.0)),
    76→            -int(action.get("count", 0)),
    77→            int(action.get("priority", 999)),
    78→        ),
    79→    )
    80→    if not ranked:
    81→        return False
    82→    has_plan = plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"))
    83→    label = "Score context:" if has_plan else "Biggest things impacting score:"
    84→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "cyan"))
    85→    for action in ranked[:limit]:
    86→        detector = action.get("detector", "unknown")
    87→        count = int(action.get("count", 0))
    88→        cluster_count = action.get("cluster_count")
    89→        if cluster_count:
    90→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open in {cluster_count} cluster(s) — `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    91→        else:
    92→            command = action.get("command", "desloppify next")
    93→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`", "dim"))
    94→    return True
    95→
    96→
    97→__all__ = [
    98→    "print_agent_plan",
    99→    "print_ranked_actions",
   100→    "print_replacement_groups",
   101→]
   102→
```

```
1→"""Skill-document versioning and install metadata helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import get_project_root
     9→
    10→# Bump this integer whenever docs/SKILL.md changes in a way that agents
    11→# should pick up (new commands, changed workflows, removed sections).
    12→SKILL_VERSION = 2
    13→
    14→SKILL_VERSION_RE = re.compile(r"<!--\s*desloppify-skill-version:\s*(\d+)\s*-->")
    15→SKILL_OVERLAY_RE = re.compile(r"<!--\s*desloppify-overlay:\s*(\w+)\s*-->")
    16→
    17→SKILL_BEGIN = "<!-- desloppify-begin -->"
    18→SKILL_END = "<!-- desloppify-end -->"
    19→
    20→# Locations where the skill doc might be installed, relative to project root.
    21→SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS = (
    22→    ".claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md",
    23→    ".opencode/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md",
    24→    "AGENTS.md",
    25→    "CLAUDE.md",
    26→    ".cursor/rules/desloppify.md",
    27→    ".github/copilot-instructions.md",
    28→)
    29→
    30→# Interface name → (target file, overlay filename, dedicated).
    31→# Dedicated files are overwritten entirely; shared files get section replacement.
    32→SKILL_TARGETS: dict[str, tuple[str, str, bool]] = {
    33→    "claude": (".claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md", "CLAUDE", True),
    34→    # OpenCode support added with thanks to @H3xKatana.
    35→    "opencode": (".opencode/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md", "OPENCODE", True),
    36→    "codex": ("AGENTS.md", "CODEX", False),
    37→    "cursor": (".cursor/rules/desloppify.md", "CURSOR", True),
    38→    "copilot": (".github/copilot-instructions.md", "COPILOT", False),
    39→    "windsurf": ("AGENTS.md", "WINDSURF", False),
    40→    "gemini": ("AGENTS.md", "GEMINI", False),
    41→}
    42→
    43→
    44→@dataclass
    45→class SkillInstall:
    46→    """Detected skill document installation."""
    47→
    48→    rel_path: str
    49→    version: int
    50→    overlay: str | None
    51→    stale: bool
    52→
    53→
    54→def find_all_installed_skills() -> list[SkillInstall]:
    55→    """Find all installed skill documents across all search paths."""
    56→    project_root = get_project_root()
    57→    results: list[SkillInstall] = []
    58→    for rel_path in SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS:
    59→        full = project_root / rel_path
    60→        if not full.is_file():
    61→            continue
    62→        try:
    63→            content = full.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    64→        except OSError:
    65→            continue
    66→        version_match = SKILL_VERSION_RE.search(content)
    67→        if not version_match:
    68→            continue
    69→        installed_version = int(version_match.group(1))
    70→        overlay_match = SKILL_OVERLAY_RE.search(content)
    71→        overlay = overlay_match.group(1) if overlay_match else None
    72→        results.append(SkillInstall(
    73→            rel_path=rel_path,
    74→            version=installed_version,
    75→            overlay=overlay,
    76→            stale=installed_version < SKILL_VERSION,
    77→        ))
    78→    return results
    79→
    80→
    81→def find_installed_skill() -> SkillInstall | None:
    82→    """Find first installed skill document metadata, or None."""
    83→    installs = find_all_installed_skills()
    84→    return installs[0] if installs else None
    85→
    86→
    87→def check_skill_version() -> str | None:
    88→    """Return a warning if any installed skill doc is outdated."""
    89→    installs = find_all_installed_skills()
    90→    stale = [i for i in installs if i.stale]
    91→    if not stale:
    92→        return None
    93→    if len(stale) == 1:
    94→        return (
    95→            f"Your desloppify skill document is outdated "
    96→            f"(v{stale[0].version}, current v{SKILL_VERSION}). "
    97→            "Run: desloppify update-skill"
    98→        )
    99→    paths = ", ".join(i.rel_path for i in stale)
   100→    return (
   101→        f"{len(stale)} skill documents are outdated "
   102→        f"(current v{SKILL_VERSION}): {paths}. "
   103→        "Run: desloppify update-skill"
   104→    )
   105→
   106→
   107→__all__ = [
   108→    "SKILL_VERSION",
   109→    "SKILL_VERSION_RE",
   110→    "SKILL_OVERLAY_RE",
   111→    "SKILL_BEGIN",
   112→    "SKILL_END",
   113→    "SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS",
   114→    "SKILL_TARGETS",
   115→    "SkillInstall",
   116→    "find_all_installed_skills",
   117→    "find_installed_skill",
   118→    "check_skill_version",
   119→]
   120→
```

```
1→"""Rendering and output helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.subjective import print_subjective_followup
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan import (
    11→    scan_reporting_dimensions as reporting_dimensions_mod,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    16→from desloppify.core.enums import canonical_finding_status
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    19→
    20→from .formatting import format_detail
    21→
    22→
    23→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    24→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    25→    try:
    26→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    27→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} findings to {output_file}", "green"))
    28→    except OSError as exc:
    29→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    30→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    31→        return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    36→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    37→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    38→    for finding in matches:
    39→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    40→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    44→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    45→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    46→    status_icon = {
    47→        "open": "○",
    48→        "fixed": "✓",
    49→        "wontfix": "—",
    50→        "false_positive": "✗",
    51→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    52→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    53→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    54→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    55→    print(
    56→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    57→    )
    58→
    59→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))
    60→    if detail_parts:
    61→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    62→    if show_code:
    63→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    64→        target_line = (
    65→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    66→        )
    67→        if target_line and finding["file"] not in (".", ""):
    68→            snippet = read_code_snippet(finding["file"], target_line)
    69→            if snippet:
    70→                print(snippet)
    71→    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    72→        print(
    73→            colorize(
    74→                f"      ⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    75→                "red",
    76→            )
    77→        )
    78→    if finding.get("note"):
    79→        print(colorize(f"      note: {finding['note']}", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize(f"      {finding['id']}", "dim"))
    81→
    82→
    83→def render_findings(
    84→    matches: list[dict],
    85→    *,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    show_code: bool,
    89→    top: int,
    90→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    91→    hidden_total: int,
    92→    noise_budget: int,
    93→    global_noise_budget: int,
    94→    budget_warning: str | None,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    """Render grouped findings and rollup summary to terminal."""
    97→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} findings matching '{pattern}'\n",
   101→            "bold",
   102→        )
   103→    )
   104→    if budget_warning:
   105→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   106→    if hidden_total:
   107→        global_label = (
   108→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   109→        )
   110→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   111→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   112→        )
   113→        print(
   114→            colorize(
   115→                (
   116→                    "  Noise budget: "
   117→                    f"{noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
   118→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   119→                ),
   120→                "dim",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   125→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   126→    remaining_findings = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   127→
   128→    for filepath, findings in shown_files:
   129→        findings.sort(
   130→            key=lambda finding: (
   131→                finding["tier"],
   132→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding["confidence"], 9),
   133→            )
   134→        )
   135→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   136→        print(
   137→            colorize(f"  {display_path}", "cyan")
   138→            + colorize(f"  ({len(findings)} findings)", "dim")
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        for finding in findings:
   142→            _print_single_finding(finding, show_code=show_code)
   143→        print()
   144→
   145→    if remaining_findings:
   146→        print(
   147→            colorize(
   148→                (
   149→                    f"  ... and {len(remaining_files)} more files "
   150→                    f"({remaining_findings} findings). "
   151→                    f"Use --top {top + 20} to see more.\n"
   152→                ),
   153→                "dim",
   154→            )
   155→        )
   156→
   157→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   158→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
   159→    for finding in matches:
   160→        by_detector[finding["detector"]] += 1
   161→        by_tier[finding["tier"]] += 1
   162→
   163→    print(colorize("  Summary:", "bold"))
   164→    print(
   165→        colorize(
   166→            (
   167→                "    By tier:     "
   168→                + ", ".join(
   169→                    f"T{tier}:{count}" for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())
   170→                )
   171→            ),
   172→            "dim",
   173→        )
   174→    )
   175→    print(
   176→        colorize(
   177→            (
   178→                "    By detector: "
   179→                + ", ".join(
   180→                    f"{detector}:{count}"
   181→                    for detector, count in sorted(
   182→                        by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1]
   183→                    )
   184→                )
   185→            ),
   186→            "dim",
   187→        )
   188→    )
   189→    if hidden_total:
   190→        print(
   191→            colorize(
   192→                (
   193→                    "    Hidden:      "
   194→                    + ", ".join(
   195→                        f"{detector}:+{count}"
   196→                        for detector, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   197→                    )
   198→                ),
   199→                "dim",
   200→            )
   201→        )
   202→    print()
   203→
   204→
   205→def show_agent_plan(
   206→    narrative: dict, matches: list[dict], *, plan: dict | None = None
   207→) -> None:
   208→    """Render a compact plan from current findings and narrative actions.
   209→
   210→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead.
   211→    """
   212→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
   213→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan as _pap
   214→        _pap(
   215→            [],
   216→            plan=plan,
   217→            header="  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next` to see your next task):",
   218→        )
   219→        return
   220→
   221→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   222→    if not actions and not matches:
   223→        return
   224→
   225→    print(
   226→        colorize(
   227→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   228→            "yellow",
   229→        )
   230→    )
   231→    if actions:
   232→        top = actions[0]
   233→        print(
   234→            colorize(
   235→                f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}",
   236→                "cyan",
   237→            )
   238→        )
   239→    elif matches:
   240→        first = matches[0]
   241→        print(
   242→            colorize(
   243→                "  Agent focus: `desloppify next --count 20` — "
   244→                f"inspect and resolve `{first.get('id', '')}`",
   245→                "cyan",
   246→            )
   247→        )
   248→
   249→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   250→        print()
   251→
   252→
   253→def show_subjective_followup(state: dict, target_strict_score: float) -> None:
   254→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   255→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   256→    if not dim_scores:
   257→        return
   258→
   259→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   260→        state,
   261→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   262→    )
   263→    if not subjective:
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   267→        state,
   268→        subjective,
   269→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   270→        max_quality_items=3,
   271→        max_integrity_items=5,
   272→    )
   273→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):
   274→        print()
   275→
   276→
   277→__all__ = [
   278→    "group_matches_by_file",
   279→    "render_findings",
   280→    "show_agent_plan",
   281→    "show_subjective_followup",
   282→    "write_show_output_file",
   283→]
   284→
```

```
    66→            )
    67→        )
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    71→        return
    72→
    73→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    74→    if stale_warning:

    76→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    77→    if skill_warning:

    79→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    80→    if config_warning:

    82→
    83→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    84→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    85→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    86→
    87→    lang = resolve_lang(args)

    99→    )
   100→    ignores = config.get("ignore", [])
```

```
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→

    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"

    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    stats: dict,
    77→    by_tier: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
    80→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
    81→    suppression: dict,
    82→    narrative: dict,
    83→    ignores: list[str],
    84→    overall_score: float | None,
    85→    objective_score: float | None,
    86→    strict_score: float | None,
    87→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    88→    plan: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> None:
    90→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    91→    open_scope = (
    92→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    93→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    94→        else None
    95→    )
    96→    write_query(
    97→        {
    98→            "command": "status",
    99→            "overall_score": overall_score,
   100→            "objective_score": objective_score,
   101→            "strict_score": strict_score,
   102→            "verified_strict_score": verified_strict_score,
   103→            "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   104→            "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   105→            "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   106→            "stats": stats,
   107→            "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   108→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   109→            "by_tier": by_tier,
   110→            "ignores": ignores,
   111→            "suppression": suppression,
   112→            "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   113→            "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   114→            "open_scope": open_scope,
   115→            "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   116→            "next_command": _status_next_command(narrative),
   117→            "narrative": narrative,
   118→            **({"plan": {
   119→                "active": True,
   120→                "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   121→                "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   122→                "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   123→                "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   124→            }} if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters") or plan.get("skipped")) else {}),
   125→        }
   126→    )

   129→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Show ignore list plus suppression accountability from recent scans."""
   131→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))
   132→    for p in ignores[:10]:
   133→        print(colorize(f"    {p}", "dim"))
   134→
   135→    last_ignored = int(suppression.get("last_ignored", 0) or 0)
   136→    last_raw = int(suppression.get("last_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   137→    last_pct = float(suppression.get("last_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   138→
   139→    if last_raw > 0:
   140→        style = "red" if last_pct >= 30 else "yellow" if last_pct >= 10 else "dim"
   141→        print(
   142→            colorize(
   143→                f"  Ignore suppression (last scan): {last_ignored}/{last_raw} findings hidden ({last_pct:.1f}%)",
   144→                style,
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    elif suppression.get("recent_scans", 0):
   148→        print(colorize("  Ignore suppression (last scan): 0 findings hidden", "dim"))
   149→
   150→    recent_scans = int(suppression.get("recent_scans", 0) or 0)
```

```
1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    33→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    34→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    35→
    36→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    37→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    38→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    39→    if total_skipped:
    40→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    41→    else:
    42→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    43→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    44→    if clusters:
    45→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    46→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    47→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    48→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    49→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    50→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    51→    if described or noted:
    52→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    53→    if active:
    54→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    55→    if superseded:
    56→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    57→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
    58→    if epics:
    59→        print(f"  Epics:            {len(epics)}")
    60→        for name, epic in epics.items():
    61→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
    62→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
    63→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
    64→            fcount = len(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
    65→            safe_tag = " [agent-safe]" if epic.get("agent_safe") else ""
    66→            print(f"    {name} [{direction}] ({fcount} findings, {status}){safe_tag}")
    67→            if thesis:
    68→                print(f"      {thesis}")
    69→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    70→    if meta and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
    71→        print(f"  Strategy:         {meta['strategy_summary']}")
    72→        print(f"  Last synthesis:   v{meta.get('version', 0)} ({meta.get('trigger', 'unknown')} @ {meta.get('last_run', 'never')})")
    73→
    74→
    75→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    76→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    77→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    78→
    79→    plan = load_plan()
    80→    reset_plan(plan)
    81→    save_plan(plan)
    82→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    83→
    84→
    85→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    86→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    87→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    88→
    89→    if plan_action is None:
    90→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    if plan_action == "show":
    94→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    if plan_action == "queue":
    98→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
    99→
   100→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
```

```
1→"""Core query payload writing helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import sys
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.core.config import config_for_query, load_config
    11→from desloppify.core.file_paths import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    13→from desloppify.state import json_default
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→
    18→def write_query(data: dict, *, query_file: Path) -> OutputResult:
    19→    """Write structured query payloads with config context and graceful fallback."""
    20→    if "config" not in data:
    21→        try:
    22→            data["config"] = config_for_query(load_config())
    23→        except (OSError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
    24→            data["config_error"] = str(exc)
    25→            logger.debug("Skipping config injection into query payload: %s", exc)
    26→    try:
    27→        safe_write_text(
    28→            query_file, json.dumps(data, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    29→        )
    30→        print("  → query.json updated", file=sys.stderr)
    31→        return OutputResult(
    32→            ok=True,
    33→            status="written",
    34→            message=f"query payload written to {query_file}",
    35→        )
    36→    except OSError as exc:
    37→        data["query_write_error"] = str(exc)
    38→        print(f"  ⚠ Could not write query.json: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
    39→        return OutputResult(
    40→            ok=False,
    41→            status="error",
    42→            message=str(exc),
    43→            error_kind="query_write_error",
    44→        )
    45→
    46→
    47→__all__ = ["write_query"]
    48→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
```

```
   171→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   172→    dim_scores: dict,
   173→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   174→) -> str | None:
   175→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""

   184→            lowest_score = strict_val
   185→            lowest_name = dim.name
   186→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   187→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   188→        if strict_val < lowest_score:

   190→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")

   194→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:

   212→        if not dimension:
```

```
463→def show_agent_plan(narrative: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
   464→    """Show concise action plan derived from narrative.actions.
   465→
   466→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead.
   467→    """
   468→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
   469→        print_agent_plan(
   470→            [],
   471→            plan=plan,
   472→            header="  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next` to see your next task):",
   473→        )
   474→        return
   475→
   476→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   477→    if not actions:
   478→        return
   479→
   480→    print(
   481→        colorize(
   482→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   483→            "yellow",
   484→        )
   485→    )
   486→    top = actions[0]
   487→    print(colorize(f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}", "cyan"))
   488→
   489→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   490→        print()
   491→
   492→
   493→def _collect_structural_areas(
   494→    state: dict,
   495→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   496→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   497→
   498→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   499→    """
   500→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   501→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   502→    )
   503→
   504→    structural = [
   505→        f
   506→        for f in findings.values()
   507→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   508→    ]
   509→
   510→    if len(structural) < 5:
   511→        return None
   512→
   513→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   514→    for f in structural:
   515→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   516→        areas[area].append(f)
   517→
   518→    if len(areas) < 2:
   519→        return None
   520→
   521→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   522→
   523→
   524→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   525→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   526→    rows = []
   527→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   528→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   529→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   530→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   531→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   532→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   533→        rows.append(
   534→            [
   535→                area,
   536→                str(len(area_findings)),
   537→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   538→                str(open_count),
   539→                str(debt_count),
   540→                str(weight),
   541→            ]
   542→        )
   543→    return rows
   544→
   545→
   546→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   547→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   548→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   549→    if remaining > 0:
   550→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   551→
   552→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   553→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   554→    print(
   555→        colorize(
   556→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   557→        )
   558→    )
   559→    print(
   560→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   561→    )
   562→    print()
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   154→    output_format: str,
   155→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   156→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   159→    renderers = {
   160→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   161→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   162→    }
   163→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   164→    if renderer is None:
   165→        return False
   166→    renderer()
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "build_query_payload",
   172→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   173→    "render_markdown",
   174→    "serialize_item",
   175→    "write_output_file",
   176→]
   177→
```

```
1→"""Contextual reminders with decay."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from datetime import UTC
     7→from datetime import datetime as _dt
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import (
    11→    _FEEDBACK_URL,
    12→    _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD,
    13→    STRUCTURAL_MERGE,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.state import StateModel, get_strict_score, path_scoped_findings

    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _compute_fp_rates(findings: dict) -> dict[tuple[str, str], float]:
    21→    """Compute false_positive rate per (detector, zone) from historical findings."""
    22→    counts: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, int]] = {}
    23→    for finding in findings.values():
    24→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    25→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    26→            detector = "structural"
    27→        zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    28→        key = (detector, zone)
    29→        if key not in counts:
    30→            counts[key] = {"total": 0, "fp": 0}
    31→        counts[key]["total"] += 1
    32→        if finding.get("status") == "false_positive":
    33→            counts[key]["fp"] += 1
    34→
    35→    rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float] = {}
    36→    for key, stat in counts.items():
    37→        if stat["total"] >= 5 and stat["fp"] > 0:
    38→            rates[key] = stat["fp"] / stat["total"]
    39→    return rates
    40→
    41→
    42→def _auto_fixer_reminder(actions: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    43→    auto_fix_actions = [
    44→        action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    45→    ]
    46→    if not auto_fix_actions:
    47→        return []
    48→    total = sum(action.get("count", 0) for action in auto_fix_actions)
    49→    if total <= 0:
    50→        return []
    51→    first_cmd = auto_fix_actions[0].get("command", "desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run")
    52→    return [
    53→        {
    54→            "type": "auto_fixers_available",
    55→            "message": f"{total} findings are auto-fixable. Run `{first_cmd}`.",
    56→            "command": first_cmd,
    57→        }
    58→    ]
    59→

    69→        }
```

```
1→"""Headline computation for terminal display."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from ._constants import _history_strict
     6→
     7→
     8→def _compute_headline(
     9→    phase: str,
    10→    dimensions: dict,
    11→    debt: dict,
    12→    milestone: str | None,
    13→    diff: dict | None,
    14→    obj_strict: float | None,
    15→    obj_score: float | None,
    16→    stats: dict,
    17→    history: list[dict],
    18→    open_by_detector: dict | None = None,
    19→) -> str | None:
    20→    """Compute one computed sentence for terminal display."""
    21→    # Security callout prefix — prepended to any headline when security findings exist
    22→    security_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("security", 0)
    23→    security_prefix = ""
    24→    if security_count > 0:
    25→        s = "s" if security_count != 1 else ""
    26→        security_prefix = f"\u26a0 {security_count} security finding{s} — review before other cleanup. "
    27→
    28→    # Review findings callout — all phases
    29→    review_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("review", 0)
    30→    review_suffix = ""
    31→    if review_count > 0:
    32→        s = "s" if review_count != 1 else ""
    33→        uninvestigated = (open_by_detector or {}).get("review_uninvestigated", 0)
    34→        if uninvestigated > 0:
    35→            review_suffix = (
    36→                f" ({review_count} review finding{s} \u2014 run `desloppify show review --status open`)"
    37→            )
    38→        else:
    39→            review_suffix = f" ({review_count} review finding{s} pending)"
    40→
    41→    headline = _compute_headline_inner(
    42→        phase, dimensions, debt, milestone, diff, obj_strict, obj_score, stats, history
    43→    )
    44→
    45→    if headline is None and not security_prefix and not review_suffix:
    46→        return None
    47→    parts = security_prefix + (headline or "") + review_suffix
    48→    return parts or None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _compute_headline_inner(
    52→    phase: str,
    53→    dimensions: dict,
    54→    debt: dict,
    55→    milestone: str | None,
    56→    diff: dict | None,
    57→    strict_score: float | None,
    58→    overall_score: float | None,
    59→    stats: dict,
    60→    history: list[dict],
    61→) -> str | None:
    62→    """Compute the base headline (without security prefix)."""
    63→    # Milestone takes priority
    64→    if milestone:
    65→        return milestone
    66→
    67→    # First scan framing
    68→    if phase == "first_scan":
    69→        dims = len(dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])) if dimensions else 0
    70→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
    71→        if dims:
    72→            return f"First scan complete. {open_count} open findings across {dims} dimensions."
    73→        return f"First scan complete. {open_count} findings detected."
    74→
    75→    # Regression — acknowledge that drops after fixes are normal
    76→    if phase == "regression":
    77→        prev = _history_strict(history[-2])
    78→        curr = _history_strict(history[-1])
    79→        if prev is not None and curr is not None:
    80→            drop = round(prev - curr, 1)
```

## Summary of UX Gaps & Confusions

| Surface | What's Missing / Confusing |
|---------|--------------------------|
| `plan done` | No epic progress update shown, no cluster context printed |
| `plan skip` | No plan context, doesn't indicate which cluster the skip affects |
| `plan focus` | No cluster size/composition preview, no progress indicators |
| Cluster commands | No dimension-aware grouping, no auto-tag explanation |
| `plan move` | No before/after position context, no visual queue state |
| Scan post-messages | No auto-cluster results reported, epics not mentioned |
| `next` rendering | Epic agent-safe flag visible but unexplained; no dependency visualization |
| `plan show` | No epic dependency structure; auto-tag not shown |
| `plan queue` | No epic dependency visualization; queue position not numeric |
| **General** | **Epics are largely invisible in the UX unless explicitly rendered by `next`/`plan show`. No nudge/coaching about epic synthesis being needed after review changes.** |

---

## Key Observations

1. **Epic visibility is conditional**: Epics only render when you explicitly call `next` or `plan show`. Agents working through `plan done` / `plan skip` won't see epic context.

2. **No synthesis nudge after scan**: Scan prints `"epic synthesis needed"` but doesn't guide agents toward `plan synthesize`.

3. **Cluster context is weak**: Items show their cluster membership in `next` (via plan_cluster dict), but `plan skip` / `plan done` don't acknowledge cluster removal.

4. **Agent-safe flag exists but undefined**: Visible in `_render_epic_item()` but no explanation of what "agent-safe" means for automation.

5. **Meta-plan is hidden**: Only shows in `next` output (via strategy_summary). Not visible in `plan show` unless you look at epic_synthesis_meta.

6. **No batch progress tracking**: When an agent resolves items in a cluster, no feedback that "cluster X is now 5% complete" or "all items in cluster X resolved!".

This is the complete UX surface exploration at maximum thoroughness.

## Comprehensive Report: Nudges and Coaching Messages Across Desloppify

Based on a thorough analysis of the codebase, here's how nudges and coaching messages work to guide agents to their next action:

---

### 1. **NARRATIVE COACHING ENGINE** (`desloppify/intelligence/narrative/`)

The narrative module is the central orchestrator for computing coaching messages. It includes:

**Core Components:**
- **`core.py`** (`compute_narrative()` entry point):
  - Computes structured narrative context from state data
  - Takes `NarrativeContext` with optional `plan` parameter (clusters dict)
  - Returns `NarrativeResult` TypedDict containing all coaching fields

- **`action_engine.py`** (action computation):
  - `compute_actions()` generates prioritized action list
  - Actions include: `auto_fix`, `reorganize`, `refactor`, `manual_fix`, `debt_review`
  - Each action has: `type`, `detector`, `count`, `description`, `command`, `impact`, `dimension`
  - Actions are sorted by type priority, then by impact descending
  - Uses cluster info to annotate actions when clusters exist

- **`strategy_engine.py`** (execution strategy):
  - `compute_strategy()` generates hint for execution approach
  - Analyzes fixer leverage (% of findings auto-fixable)
  - Partitions actions into parallelizable "lanes" by file overlap
  - Returns `hint` string explaining strategy (e.g., "Run fixers first — they cover 80% of findings. Then 2 independent workstreams, safe to parallelize.")

- **`headline.py`** (status summary):
  - `_compute_headline()` generates one-sentence summary
  - Detects phase (first_scan, regression, stagnation, maintenance)
  - Prefixes security callouts, appends review findings callouts

- **`reminders.py`** (contextual coaching):
  - Auto-fixer availability reminders
  - Rescan-needed nudges (post-fix)
  - Badge setup reminders (when score ≥90 and scorecard exists but not in README)
  - False positive pattern warnings
  - Reopen count warnings

**Output TypedDict `NarrativeResult`:**
```
{
  "phase": str,                    # first_scan|regression|stagnation|maintenance
  "headline": str | None,          # One-sentence summary with security/review callouts
  "actions": list[ActionItem],     # Prioritized action list with commands
  "strategy": dict,                # {fixer_leverage, lanes, can_parallelize, hint}
  "primary_action": dict,          # Top action: {command, description}
  "why_now": str,                  # Explanation for recommended action timing
  "verification_step": dict,       # {command: "desloppify scan", reason}
  "strict_target": dict,           # {target, current, gap, state, warning}
  "reminders": list,               # Contextual coaching items with decay
  "reminder_history": dict,        # Show counts for reminder decay (suppressed after 3)
  ... (dimensions, debt, risk_flags, milestone, tools, badge status)
}
```

**Key Pattern:** Narrative receives `plan.get("clusters")` and uses it to annotate actions with cluster info for guiding agents toward batch work.

---

### 2. **SCAN LLM COACHING BLOCK** (`desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_llm.py`)

When an agent environment is detected (CLAUDE_CODE, DESLOPPIFY_AGENT, etc.), the `_print_llm_summary()` function generates structured coaching:

**What It Shows (in order):**
1. **Score legend** — explains overall/objective/strict/verified scoring models
2. **Dimension table** — markdown table with Health|Strict|Issues|Tier|Action columns
3. **Drag summary** — top 5 dimensions with biggest score impact (sorted by drag)
4. **Stats summary** — total|open|fixed|wontfix counts, plus ignore suppression
5. **Living plan announcement** (if active):
   - `{ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped` queue status
   - Focused cluster (if any) with remaining count
   - Direction: "Use `desloppify next`" and "`desloppify plan` to view and update"
6. **Workflow guide** — full cycle instructions (scan → show → fix → scan)
7. **Narrative status** — current headline, strategy hint, top action
8. **Badge hint** — if scorecard.png exists, suggests README embedding

**Code Location:** Lines 258-338 in `scan_reporting_llm.py`. The "INSTRUCTIONS FOR LLM" section (lines 286-290) emphasizes: "ALWAYS present ALL scores to the user after a scan. ... The goal is to maximize strict scores."

---

### 3. **`next` COMMAND FOLLOWUP NUDGES** (`desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`)

The `next` command renders contextual follow-up guidance:

**Tier Navigator** (`_render_tier_navigator()`):
- Shows open count per tier: `T1:5 T2:10 T3:20 T4:30`
- Suggests: `desloppify next --tier 1|2|3|4`

**Strategy Summary** (`_render_strategy_summary()`):
- Shows `epic_synthesis_meta.strategy_summary` when epics exist
- Printed as indented dim text under "Meta-plan:" header

**Epic/Synthesis Cards** (`_render_epic_item()`, `_render_synthesis_item()`):
- **Epic cards** show: thesis, root cause, status, dependency order, action steps, finding IDs
  - Bottom hint: `desloppify plan focus {epic_name}` to work on it
- **Synthesis card** shows: review findings delta, epic status breakdown, triggers synthesis
  - Action: `desloppify plan synthesize` or `--dry-run`

**Followup Nudges** (`render_followup_nudges()`):
- **North star line:** shows strict score gap to target (`+2.3 needed`)
- **Subjective rollup:** `{N} dimensions below target, {S} stale, {U} unassessed, {R} review findings open, {C} files need review`
- Suggests: `desloppify show subjective` for details

**Item Impact Hints:**
- Shows dimension drag for review findings (e.g., "costs −0.15 pts on overall score")
- Shows bulk impact estimate for auto-fixable batches

---

### 4. **QUERY.JSON OUTPUT** (`desloppify/app/commands/helpers/query.py`, `next_parts/output.py`)

What gets written to `.desloppify/query.json` by `next` command:

**Payload Structure:**
```json
{
  "command": "next",
  "items": [
    {
      "id": "...",
      "kind": "finding|cluster|epic|synthesis_needed|subjective_dimension",
      "tier": 1,
      "effective_tier": 1,
      "confidence": "high",
      "detector": "unused",
      "file": "path/to/file.py",
      "summary": "...",
      "detail": {...},
      "status": "open",
      "primary_command": "desloppify fix ...",
      // Plan metadata (if plan overrides):
      "queue_position": 5,
      "plan_description": "...",
      "plan_note": "...",
      "plan_cluster": {name, description, total_items},
      "plan_skipped": false
    }
  ],
  "queue": {
    "tier_counts": {1: 5, 2: 10, ...},
    "requested_tier": 1,
    "selected_tier": 1,
    "fallback_reason": null,
    "available_tiers": [1, 2, 3, 4],
    "total": 100
  },
  "narrative": {
    "phase": "...",
    "headline": "...",
    "actions": [{type, command, description, detector, count, impact, ...}],
    "strategy": {hint: "...", fixer_leverage: {...}, lanes: {...}, ...},
    "strict_target": {target: 95, current: 87.5, gap: 7.5, state: "below", ...},
    // ... all other narrative fields
  },
  "plan": {
    "active": true,
    "focus": "cluster-name",
    "clusters": [{name, description, item_count}, ...],
    "total_ordered": 50,
    "total_skipped": 10,
    "plan_overrides_narrative": true
  }
}
```

**Key Insight:** The `plan` field only appears if the living plan is active (has queue_order, clusters, or skipped items). This signals to agents: "The plan is the single source of truth for work order."

---

### 5. **SKILL.MD DOCUMENTATION** (`desloppify/core/skill_docs.py`)

The skill document is a versioned agent instruction file. Metadata:

- **Version tracking:** `SKILL_VERSION = 2` (bumped when commands/workflows change)
- **Install locations:** `.claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md`, `.cursor/rules/desloppify.md`, `AGENTS.md`, etc.
- **Version marker in doc:** `<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->`
- **Staleness check:** Warns if installed version < current version
- **Auto-update:** `scan` command attempts to auto-update via `_try_auto_update_skill()`

**What the SKILL.md contains:**
- Command reference (scan, show, fix, next, plan, etc.)
- Workflow patterns (scan → show → fix → scan)
- Plan usage (synthesize, focus, done, skip)
- Epic/synthesis concepts
- Attestation examples

---

### 6. **STATUS COMMAND** (`desloppify/app/commands/status_cmd.py`, `status_parts/render.py`)

The `status` command shows the score dashboard with contextual coaching:

**Terminal Output:**
1. Score summary lines with target vs. current gap
2. Scan metrics (total findings, open, fixed, wontfix)
3. Open scope breakdown (by zone, by area)
4. Dimension health table (with focus arrow on lowest dimension)
5. Score model breakdown (mechanical vs. subjective breakdown)
6. Drag summary (biggest score-drag dimensions)
7. Structural areas (T3/T4 findings grouped by codebase area)
8. **Agent plan** (`show_agent_plan()`):
   - If living plan is active: shows focus + total_ordered/total_skipped
   - Otherwise: shows top action from narrative with ranked action list
9. Review summary + subjective followup
10. Headline (from narrative)

**Query Output:**
The `write_status_query()` function writes to query.json:
```json
{
  "command": "status",
  "overall_score": 87.5,
  "strict_score": 82.1,
  "dimension_scores": {...},
  "stats": {...},
  "narrative": {...},
  "plan": {
    "active": true,
    "focus": "cluster-name",
    "total_ordered": 50,
    "total_skipped": 10,
    "plan_overrides_narrative": true
  }
}
```

**Key Pattern:** Narrative is computed with `command="status"` and `plan=_plan_active`, so the narrative adapts when a plan is in use.

---

### 7. **SHOW COMMAND** (`desloppify/app/commands/show/render.py`)

The `show` command renders findings with contextual guidance:

**Agent Plan Block** (`show_agent_plan()`):
- If living plan is active: shows plan focus/progress
- Otherwise: shows top action from narrative + ranked action list
- Format: `Agent focus: desloppify fix <fixer> — <description>`

**Impact Estimates:**
- Shows dimension drag for review findings (how many points fixing it recovers)
- Shows bulk impact for auto-fixable batches

**Plan Metadata on Items:**
- If item has plan overrides: shows description, cluster name, note
- Shows queue position if available

---

### 8. **PLAN SYNTHESIS & EPICS** (`desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py`)

The `plan show` subcommand displays:

```
Living Plan Status
  Queue:            50 items prioritized
  Skipped:          10 (temp: 3, wontfix: 5, fp: 2)
  Clusters:         5
    cluster-1: 20 items — "Fix async patterns"
    cluster-2: 15 items — "Refactor error handling"
  Annotations:      8 described, 12 noted
  Focus:            cluster-2
  Epics:            3
    epic-1 [refactoring] (25 findings, completed) [agent-safe]
      Root cause: Legacy async patterns
    epic-2 [docs] (10 findings, in_progress) [agent-safe]
      Thesis: Add JSDoc coverage
  Strategy:         {strategy_summary from epic_synthesis_meta}
  Last synthesis:   v4 (user_synthesize @ 2026-03-01 10:15:00)
```

**Epic Metadata** includes:
- `direction` (e.g., "refactoring", "docs", "testing")
- `thesis` (why this work matters)
- `root_cause` (what caused the issue)
- `status` (pending|in_progress|completed)
- `agent_safe` (boolean — is this safe for agent execution?)
- `dependency_order` (position in epic sequence)
- `finding_ids` (which issues belong to this epic)
- `action_steps` (breakdown of what to do)

---

## SUMMARY: Current Gaps & Opportunities

### What's Currently Shown:
✅ **In Terminal:**
- Narrative actions with commands and descriptions
- Strategy hint (parallelization advice)
- Primary action (top priority)
- Strict score target vs. current gap
- Reminders (auto-fixer availability, rescan needs, badge tips)
- Epic/synthesis cards (in `next` output)
- Plan focus + total counts (when plan is active)
- Headline with phase context

✅ **In query.json:**
- Full narrative structure (all fields listed above)
- Living plan metadata (active, focus, clusters summary, total_ordered/skipped)
- Items with plan metadata (queue_position, plan_description, plan_note, plan_cluster)
- Tier navigator info
- Strategy lanes and fixer leverage

### What's MISSING or Underdeveloped:
❌ **Not in narrative:** Epics/synthesis strategy only appear in `plan show` and in `next` rendered output, NOT in the NarrativeResult TypedDict. Agents don't get epic info through the standard narrative coaching flow.

❌ **Status command:** Doesn't show epic synthesis summary or next epics to work on. Uses narrative.actions instead of plan epics.

❌ **Show command:** Shows findings but doesn't highlight which epic they belong to (if any).

❌ **SKILL.md:** Not versioned dynamically based on plan/epic features. Version bumps are manual.

❌ **Synthesis timing nudges:** No nudge in query.json or narrative saying "synthesis needed" with trigger reason.

❌ **Per-epic success metrics:** No guidance on "this epic is 60% done, next step is X".

---

This architecture enables agents to understand not just what to do next, but *why* and *how* to do it efficiently.

> DEVELOPER

Based on thorough exploration of the desloppify codebase, I need to design a plan to make the epic synthesis system's UX coherent and obvious. Here's the context:

## Problem Statement

The epic synthesis system was implemented (schema, queue injection, work queue, next rendering, plan synthesize command) but the UX is fragmented. An agent working through the plan doesn't get consistent guidance about epics at every touch point. Specific issues:

### Critical gaps (workflow breaks)

1. **SKILL.md doesn't mention `plan synthesize`** — agents don't know the command exists. File: `docs/SKILL.md` (123 lines). The "To be strategic" section lists plan commands but not synthesize. Need to add epic synthesis to both the workflow section and the command reference.

2. **Scan LLM block doesn't show strategy summary** — after scan, agents see "LIVING PLAN ACTIVE" but no epic/strategy context. File: `desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_llm.py` lines 304-315. When epics exist, should show `epic_synthesis_meta.strategy_summary` and epic counts.

3. **Synthesis hidden when focus is set** — when `plan focus <cluster>` is active, synthesis::pending disappears from queue because it's not in the cluster. Agents lose awareness. File: `desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` `render_terminal_items()` (line 459). When focus is active AND synthesis is pending, should show a header note like "Note: synthesis::pending is waiting — run `desloppify plan synthesize` first."

4. **No epic progress in `plan done`/resolve** — resolving a finding in an epic gives zero feedback about epic progress. Files: `desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py` lines 91-102 (plan purge), `desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`. After resolving, should check if resolved finding was in an epic and show "Epic epic/delete-facades: 5/8 → 4/8 remaining."

### Important gaps (confusing without)

5. **`plan show` is overwhelming** — 56 clusters listed in full. File: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py` `_cmd_plan_show()` lines 18-72. Auto-clusters should be collapsed into a summary like "40 auto-clusters (755 findings)" with only manual clusters and epics shown in detail.

6. **`cluster show` doesn't show annotations** — file: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py` `_cmd_cluster_show()` lines 138-175. Per-member annotations (description, note) from plan overrides aren't shown.

7. **Synthesis nudge after `plan done`/`plan skip`** — when resolving a review finding, the meta-plan may go stale. Should print "Synthesis may be stale — run `desloppify plan synthesize`" if the finding was a review finding and synthesis::pending is not already in queue.

### Nice to have

8. **Queue table wider cluster column** — file: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py` line 152. Currently 16 chars, too narrow for cluster names.

9. **Status command shows epic summary** — `desloppify/app/commands/status_parts/render.py` show_agent_plan.

## Key files and code structure

- **SKILL.md**: `docs/SKILL.md` — 123 lines, template installed to `.claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md` etc. Version is `SKILL_VERSION = 2` in `desloppify/core/skill_docs.py`.
- **Scan LLM block**: `desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_llm.py` `_print_llm_summary()` lines 258-338.
- **Next render**: `desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` — `render_terminal_items()`, `_render_strategy_summary()`, `_render_synthesis_item()`, `_render_epic_item()`, `render_followup_nudges()`.
- **Resolve flow**: `desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py` `cmd_resolve()` — loads plan, purges IDs at lines 91-102.
- **Override handlers**: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py` — `cmd_plan_done()` delegates to `cmd_resolve()`, `cmd_plan_skip()` saves plan at line 151.
- **Plan show**: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py` `_cmd_plan_show()`.
- **Cluster show**: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py` `_cmd_cluster_show()`.
- **Queue render**: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py` line 152 widths.
- **Plan facade**: `desloppify/engine/plan.py` — re-exports from `_plan/*`.
- **Schema**: `desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py` — `PlanModel`, `Epic`, `EpicSynthesisMeta`.
- **Stale dimensions**: `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` — `SYNTHESIS_ID`, `sync_synthesis_needed()`, `review_finding_snapshot_hash()`.

## Design constraints

- Keep changes minimal — each fix should be ~5-20 lines
- Follow existing patterns (e.g., colorize calls, print formatting, facade imports)
- Don't add new modules — extend existing files
- SKILL_VERSION should be bumped to 3 since we're adding new workflow commands
- Test changes by running the tool manually against the desloppify codebase

Please design a concrete implementation plan with exact file paths, function names, and the specific output strings that will be printed. Group into priority tiers (critical, important, nice-to-have).

For the epic progress in resolve flow, the logic should be: after purging IDs from plan, check plan.epics for any epic that contained one of the resolved IDs. If found, compute remaining = len(epic.finding_ids). Print "Epic {name}: {remaining} findings remaining" (or "Epic {name}: completed!" if 0).

For the synthesis staleness nudge, the logic should be: after plan done/skip of a review finding, check if SYNTHESIS_ID is in plan.queue_order. If not, and the finding was a review finding, print the nudge.

1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Improve code quality by maximising the **strict score** honestly.
    19→
    20→**The main thing you do is run `desloppify next`** — it tells you exactly what to fix and how. Fix it, resolve it, run `next` again. Keep going.
    21→
    22→Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    23→
    24→## 2. The Workflow
    25→
    26→Two loops. The **outer loop** rescans periodically to measure progress.
    27→The **inner loop** is where you spend most of your time: fixing issues one by one.
    28→
    29→### Outer loop — scan and check
    30→
    31→```bash
    32→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    33→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    34→```
    35→If not at target, work the inner loop. Rescan periodically — especially after clearing a cluster or batch of related fixes. Issues cascade-resolve and new ones may surface.
    36→
    37→### Inner loop — fix issues
    38→
    39→Repeat until the queue is clear:
    40→
    41→```
    42→1. desloppify next              ← tells you exactly what to fix next
    43→2. Fix the issue in code
    44→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    45→```
    46→
    47→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    48→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    49→
    50→**To be strategic**, use `plan` to shape what `next` gives you:
    51→```bash
    52→desloppify plan                        # see the full ordered queue
    53→desloppify plan move <pat> top         # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    54→desloppify plan cluster create <name>  # group related issues to batch-fix
    55→desloppify plan focus <cluster>        # scope next to one cluster
    56→desloppify plan defer <pat>            # push low-value items aside
    57→desloppify plan skip <pat>             # hide from next
    58→desloppify plan done <pat>             # mark complete
    59→desloppify plan reopen <pat>           # reopen
    60→```
    61→
    62→### Subjective reviews
    63→
    64→The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions.
    65→If you need to trigger one manually:
    66→```bash
    67→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    68→```
    69→
    70→### Other useful commands
    71→
    72→```bash
    73→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    74→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    75→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    76→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    77→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" # accept debt (lowers strict score)
    78→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    79→```
    80→
    81→## 3. Reference
    82→
    83→### How scoring works
    84→
    85→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
    86→
    87→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
    88→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
    89→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
    90→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
    91→
    92→### Subjective reviews in detail
    93→
    94→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
    95→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
    96→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
    97→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
    98→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
    99→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
   100→
   101→### Key concepts
   102→
   103→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   104→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   105→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   106→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   107→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   108→
   109→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   110→
   111→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   112→
   113→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   114→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   115→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   116→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   117→
   118→## Prerequisite
   119→
   120→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   121→
   122→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   123→
