   118→        # File distribution
   119→        from collections import Counter
   120→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   121→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   122→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   123→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   124→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   125→        else:
   126→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   127→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   128→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   129→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   130→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   131→
   132→        # Sample IDs
   133→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   134→        for m in members[:3]:
   135→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   136→        if len(members) > 3:
   137→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   138→
   139→    # Action
   140→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   141→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   142→    if primary_command:
   143→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   144→
   145→    # Resolution hints
   146→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" '
   147→                   f'--note "<what>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"', "dim"))
   148→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   149→                   "dim"))
   150→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   151→
   152→
   153→def _render_item(
   154→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   155→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   158→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   159→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   163→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   164→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   165→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   166→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   167→
   168→    # Effort/type label
   169→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   170→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   171→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   172→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   173→
   174→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   175→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   176→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   177→        subjective_score = float(
   178→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   179→        )
   180→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   181→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")
   182→        print(
   183→            colorize(
   184→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   185→                "cyan",
   186→            )
   187→        )
   188→        print(colorize(
   189→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   190→            "dim",
   191→        ))
   192→        if explain:
   193→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   194→                "policy",
   195→                "subjective items are fixed at T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3.",
   196→            )
   197→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   198→        return
   199→
   200→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   201→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   202→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   203→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   204→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   205→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   206→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   207→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   208→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   209→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   210→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   211→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   212→
   213→    print(f"  File: {item.get('file', '')}")
   214→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   215→
   216→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   217→    if detail.get("lines"):
   218→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   219→    if detail.get("category"):
   220→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   221→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   222→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   223→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   224→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   225→
   226→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   227→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   228→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   229→        if snippet:
   230→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   231→            print(snippet)
   232→
   233→    if dim_scores:
   234→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   235→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   236→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   237→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   238→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   239→            print(
   240→                colorize(
   241→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   242→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   243→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   244→                    "dim",
   245→                )
   246→            )
   247→
   248→    # Score impact estimate
   249→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   250→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   251→        try:
   252→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_score_impact
   253→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   254→            if impact > 0:
   255→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   256→            else:
   257→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   258→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   259→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   260→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   261→                    if issues > 1:
   262→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   263→                        if bulk > 0:
   264→                            print(colorize(
   265→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   266→                                "cyan",
   267→                            ))
   268→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   269→            pass
   270→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   271→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   272→        try:
   273→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
   274→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   275→            if dim_key:
   276→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   277→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   278→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   279→                        continue
   280→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   281→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   282→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   283→                        if drag > 0.01:
   284→                            print(colorize(
   285→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   286→                                "cyan",
   287→                            ))
   288→                        break
   289→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   290→            pass
   291→
   292→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   293→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   294→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   295→        similar_count = sum(
   296→            1
   297→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   298→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   299→        )
   300→        if similar_count > 1:
   301→            print(
   302→                colorize(
   303→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   304→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   305→                    "cyan",
   306→                )
   307→            )
   308→    if explain:
   309→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   310→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   311→        base = (
   312→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   313→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   314→        )
   315→
   316→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   317→        if dim_scores and detector:
   318→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   319→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   320→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   321→                base += (
   322→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   323→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   324→                )
   325→
   326→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   327→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   328→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   329→            if dim_key:
   330→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   331→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   332→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   333→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   334→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   335→                        break
   336→
   337→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   338→        if policy:
   339→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   340→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   341→
   342→
   343→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   344→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   345→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   346→        return
   347→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   348→    if not explain:
   349→        return
   350→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   351→    if available:
   352→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   353→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   354→
   355→
   356→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   357→    if queue.get("items"):
   358→        return False
   359→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   360→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   361→    if tier is not None:
   362→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   363→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   364→        if available:
   365→            commands = " | ".join(
   366→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   367→            )
   368→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   369→    return True
   370→
   371→
   372→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   373→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   374→    if not plan:
   375→        return
   376→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   377→    summary = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   378→    if not summary:
   379→        return
   380→    print(colorize("\n  Meta-plan:", "bold"))
   381→    for line in summary.strip().splitlines():
   382→        print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
   383→
   384→
   385→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   386→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   387→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   388→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   389→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   390→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   391→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   392→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   393→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   394→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   395→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   396→
   397→    print(colorize("  (Meta-plan update needed, T1)", "bold"))
   398→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   399→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   400→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   401→    print()
   402→    if new:
   403→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   404→    if resolved:
   405→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   406→    if epic_count:
   407→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   408→    print()
   409→    print("  Action:   desloppify plan synthesize")
   410→    print("  Preview:  desloppify plan synthesize --dry-run")
   411→    print()
   412→    print("  View plan:")
   413→    print("    desloppify plan queue              — see all items in priority order")
   414→    print("    desloppify plan show               — plan status summary")
   415→
   416→
   417→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   418→    """Render an epic card."""
   419→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   420→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   421→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   422→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   423→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   424→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   425→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   426→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   427→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   428→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   429→
   430→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   431→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   432→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   433→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   434→    if root_cause:
   435→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   436→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   437→    if total_epics:
   438→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   439→    print()
   440→    if action_steps:
   441→        print("  Steps:")
   442→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   443→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   444→        print()
   445→    if finding_ids:
   446→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   447→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   448→        for fid in shown:
   449→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   450→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   451→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   452→        print()
   453→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   454→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   455→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   456→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   457→
   458→
   459→def render_terminal_items(
   460→    items: list[dict],
   461→    dim_scores: dict,
   462→    findings_scoped: dict,
   463→    *,
   464→    group: str,
   465→    explain: bool,
   466→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   467→    plan: dict | None = None,
   468→) -> None:
   469→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   470→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   471→
   472→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   473→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   474→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   475→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   476→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   477→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   478→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   479→
   480→    if group != "item":
   481→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   482→        return
   483→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   484→        if idx > 0:
   485→            print()
   486→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   487→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   488→
   489→        # Special rendering for synthesis and epic items
   490→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   491→            _render_synthesis_item(item)
   492→            continue
   493→        if kind == "epic":
   494→            _render_epic_item(item)
   495→            continue
   496→
   497→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   498→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   499→        elif len(items) > 1:
   500→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   501→        else:
   502→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   503→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   504→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   505→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   506→
   507→
   508→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   509→    if len(items) != 1:
   510→        return
   511→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   512→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   513→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   514→    if kind != "finding":
   515→        return
   516→    item = items[0]
   517→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   518→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   519→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   520→        primary = item.get(
   521→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   522→        )
   523→        print(f"    {primary}")
   524→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   525→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   526→        return
   527→
   528→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   529→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   530→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   531→        print(f"    {primary}")
   532→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   533→    else:
   534→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   535→
   536→    print(
   537→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   538→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   539→    )
   540→    print(
   541→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   542→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   543→    )
   544→
   545→
   546→def render_followup_nudges(
   547→    state: dict,
   548→    dim_scores: dict,
   549→    findings_scoped: dict,
   550→    *,
   551→    strict_score: float | None,
   552→    target_strict_score: float,
   553→) -> None:
   554→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   555→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   556→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   557→        state,
   558→        subjective_entries,
   559→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   560→        max_quality_items=3,
   561→        max_integrity_items=5,
   562→    )
   563→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   564→    if strict_score is not None:
   565→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   566→        if gap > 0:
   567→            print(
   568→                colorize(
   569→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   570→                    "cyan",
   571→                )
   572→            )
   573→        else:
   574→            print(
   575→                colorize(
   576→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   577→                    "green",
   578→                )
   579→            )
   580→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   581→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   582→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   583→
   584→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   585→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   586→        findings_scoped
   587→    )
   588→    parts: list[str] = []
   589→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   590→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   591→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   592→    open_review = [
   593→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   594→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   595→    ]
   596→    if low_dims:
   597→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   598→    if stale_count:
   599→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   600→    if unassessed_count:
   601→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   602→    if len(open_review):
   603→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   604→    if coverage_open > 0:
   605→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   606→
   607→    if parts:
   608→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   609→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   610→
   611→
   612→__all__ = [
   613→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   614→    "render_followup_nudges",
   615→    "render_queue_header",
   616→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   617→    "render_terminal_items",
   618→    "scorecard_subjective",
   619→    "show_empty_queue",
   620→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   621→]
   622→

    40→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    41→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    42→    try:
    43→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    44→    except OSError as exc:
    45→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    46→        sys.exit(1)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    50→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    51→    try:
    52→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    53→    except OSError as exc:
    54→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    55→        sys.exit(1)
    56→
    57→
    58→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    59→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    60→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    61→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    62→
    63→    state_file = state_path(args)
    64→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    65→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    66→        state,
    67→        args,
    68→        attestation=attestation,
    69→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    70→    )
    71→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    72→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    73→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    74→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    75→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    79→    if not all_resolved:
    80→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    81→        print(
    82→            colorize(
    83→                f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}",
    84→                "yellow",
    85→            )
    86→        )
    87→        return
    88→
    89→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    90→
    91→    # Remove resolved items from the living plan queue (best-effort)
    92→    try:
    93→        from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan, purge_ids, save_plan
    94→
    95→        if has_living_plan():
    96→            plan = load_plan()
    97→            purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    98→            if purged:
    99→                save_plan(plan)
   100→                print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   101→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   102→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
   103→
   104→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   105→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   106→        state,
   107→        status=args.status,
   108→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   109→    )
   110→    has_review_findings = any(
   111→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   112→        for fid in all_resolved
   113→    )
   114→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
   115→
   116→    _resolve_config = config_mod.load_config()
   117→    _resolve_target = target_strict_score_from_config(_resolve_config, fallback=95.0)
   118→    _print_score_movement(
   119→        status=args.status,
   120→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   121→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   122→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   123→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   124→        state=state,
   125→        has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
   126→        target_strict=_resolve_target,
   127→    )
   128→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   129→        args=args,
   130→        state=state,
   131→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   132→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   133→    )
   134→
   135→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   136→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   137→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   138→        state,
   139→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   140→    )
   141→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   142→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   143→
   144→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   145→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   146→        ResolveQueryContext(
   147→            patterns=args.patterns,
   148→            status=args.status,
   149→            resolved=all_resolved,
   150→            next_command=next_command,
   151→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   152→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   153→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   154→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   155→            attestation=attestation,
   156→            narrative=narrative,
   157→            state=state,
   158→        )
   159→    )

   162→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   163→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""
   164→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   165→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   166→        show_attestation_requirement("Ignore", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   167→        sys.exit(1)
   168→
   169→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   170→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   171→    state = runtime.state
   172→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   173→
   174→    config = runtime.config
   175→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
   176→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
   177→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
   178→
   179→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, args.pattern)
   180→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
   181→        {
   182→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
   183→            "command": "ignore",
   184→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   185→            "attestation": attestation,
   186→            "affected": removed,
   187→        }
   188→    )
   189→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   190→
   191→    print(colorize(f"Added ignore pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
   192→    if removed:
   193→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching findings from state.")
   194→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   195→    if config_warning:
   196→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   197→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   198→
   199→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   200→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   201→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   202→        state,
   203→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="ignore"),
   204→    )
   205→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   206→    write_query(
   207→        {
   208→            "command": "ignore",
   209→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   210→            "removed": removed,
   211→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
   212→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
   213→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
   214→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   215→            "attestation": attestation,
   216→            "narrative": narrative,
   217→        }
   218→    )

     2→

    22→def cmd_plan_describe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    """Set augmented description on findings."""
    24→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    25→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    26→        return
    27→
    28→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    29→    text: str = getattr(args, "text", "")
    30→
    31→    from desloppify.engine.plan import describe_finding
    32→
    33→    plan = load_plan()
    34→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    35→    if not finding_ids:
    36→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    for fid in finding_ids:
    40→        describe_finding(plan, fid, text or None)
    41→    save_plan(plan)
    42→    print(colorize(f"  Set description on {len(finding_ids)} finding(s).", "green"))
    43→
    44→
    45→def cmd_plan_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    48→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    49→        return
    50→
    51→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])

    53→
    54→    from desloppify.engine.plan import annotate_finding
    55→
    56→    plan = load_plan()
    57→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)

    59→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))

    61→
    62→    for fid in finding_ids:

    72→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    73→    """Skip findings — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
    74→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.selection import (
    75→        show_attestation_requirement,
    76→        validate_attestation,
    77→    )
    78→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    79→
    80→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    81→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    82→        return
    83→
    84→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    85→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
    86→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
    87→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
    88→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
    89→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
    90→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    91→
    92→    # Determine skip kind
    93→    if false_positive:
    94→        kind = "false_positive"
    95→    elif permanent:
    96→        kind = "permanent"
    97→    else:
    98→        kind = "temporary"
    99→
   100→    # Validate requirements for permanent/false_positive
   101→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   102→        if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   103→            show_attestation_requirement(
   104→                "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   105→                attestation,
   106→                ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   107→            )
   108→            return
   109→        if kind == "permanent" and not note:
   110→            print(
   111→                colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   112→                file=sys.stderr,
   113→            )
   114→            return
   115→
   116→    plan = load_plan()
   117→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   118→    if not finding_ids:
   119→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   123→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   124→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   125→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   126→
   127→        state_file = state_path(args)
   128→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   129→        status = "wontfix" if kind == "permanent" else "false_positive"
   130→        resolved: list[str] = []
   131→        for fid in finding_ids:
   132→            resolved.extend(
   133→                state_mod.resolve_findings(
   134→                    state_data, fid, status, note or "", attestation=attestation
   135→                )
   136→            )
   137→        if resolved:
   138→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   139→
   140→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   141→    count = skip_items(
   142→        plan,
   143→        finding_ids,
   144→        kind=kind,
   145→        reason=reason,
   146→        note=note,
   147→        attestation=attestation,
   148→        review_after=review_after,
   149→        scan_count=scan_count,
   150→    )
   151→    save_plan(plan)
   152→
   153→    label = {"temporary": "Skipped", "permanent": "Wontfixed", "false_positive": "Marked false positive"}
   154→    print(colorize(f"  {label[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   155→    if review_after:
   156→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   157→
   158→
   159→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   160→    """Unskip findings — bring back to queue."""
   161→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   162→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   163→        return
   164→
   165→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   166→
   167→    plan = load_plan()
   168→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   169→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   170→    if not finding_ids:
   171→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
   175→    save_plan(plan)
   176→
   177→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   178→    if need_reopen:
   179→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   180→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   181→
   182→        state_file = state_path(args)
   183→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   184→        reopened: list[str] = []
   185→        for fid in need_reopen:
   186→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, fid, "open"))
   187→        if reopened:
   188→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   189→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s) in state.", "dim"))
   190→
   191→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   192→
   193→
   194→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   195→# Reopen
   196→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   197→
   198→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   199→    """Reopen resolved findings from plan context."""
   200→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   201→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   202→
   203→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   204→
   205→    state_file = state_path(args)
   206→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   207→
   208→    reopened: list[str] = []
   209→    for pattern in patterns:
   210→        reopened.extend(
   211→            state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, pattern, "open")
   212→        )
   213→
   214→    if not reopened:
   215→        print(colorize("  No resolved findings matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   216→        return
   217→
   218→    state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   219→
   220→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   221→    plan = load_plan()
   222→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   223→    count = 0
   224→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   225→    for fid in reopened:
   226→        if fid in skipped:
   227→            skipped.pop(fid)
   228→            count += 1
   229→        if fid not in order:
   230→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   231→            order.add(fid)
   232→            count += 1
   233→    if count:
   234→        save_plan(plan)
   235→
   236→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s).", "green"))
   237→    if count:
   238→        print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   239→
   240→
   241→def cmd_plan_done(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   242→    """Mark findings as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   243→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.selection import (
   244→        show_attestation_requirement,
   245→        validate_attestation,
   246→    )
   247→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
   248→
   249→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   250→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   251→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   252→
   253→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   254→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   255→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan done", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   256→        return
   257→
   258→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   259→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   260→        status="fixed",
   261→        patterns=patterns,
   262→        note=note,
   263→        attest=attestation,
   264→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   265→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   266→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   267→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   268→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   269→    )
   270→
   271→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd import cmd_resolve
   272→
   273→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)

   276→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   277→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   278→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)

   280→
   281→    plan = load_plan()
   282→    if clear_flag:
   283→        clear_focus(plan)
   284→        save_plan(plan)
   285→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   286→        return
   287→
   288→    if not cluster_name:

   290→        if active:

   292→        else:
   293→            print("  No active focus.")

1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    33→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    34→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    35→
    36→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    37→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    38→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    39→    if total_skipped:
    40→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    41→    else:
    42→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    43→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    44→    if clusters:
    45→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    46→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    47→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    48→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    49→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    50→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    51→    if described or noted:
    52→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    53→    if active:
    54→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    55→    if superseded:
    56→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    57→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
    58→    if epics:
    59→        print(f"  Epics:            {len(epics)}")
    60→        for name, epic in epics.items():
    61→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
    62→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
    63→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
    64→            fcount = len(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
    65→            safe_tag = " [agent-safe]" if epic.get("agent_safe") else ""
    66→            print(f"    {name} [{direction}] ({fcount} findings, {status}){safe_tag}")
    67→            if thesis:
    68→                print(f"      {thesis}")
    69→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    70→    if meta and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
    71→        print(f"  Strategy:         {meta['strategy_summary']}")
    72→        print(f"  Last synthesis:   v{meta.get('version', 0)} ({meta.get('trigger', 'unknown')} @ {meta.get('last_run', 'never')})")
    73→
    74→
    75→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    76→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    77→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    78→
    79→    plan = load_plan()
    80→    reset_plan(plan)
    81→    save_plan(plan)
    82→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    83→
    84→
    85→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    86→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    87→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    88→
    89→    if plan_action is None:
    90→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    if plan_action == "show":
    94→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    if plan_action == "queue":
    98→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
    99→
   100→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    if plan_action == "reset":
   104→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   105→        return
   106→
   107→    if plan_action == "move":
   108→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
   109→
   110→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   111→        return
   112→
   113→    if plan_action in (
   114→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   115→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   116→    ):
   117→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
   118→            cmd_plan_describe,
   119→            cmd_plan_done,
   120→            cmd_plan_focus,
   121→            cmd_plan_note,
   122→            cmd_plan_reopen,
   123→            cmd_plan_skip,
   124→            cmd_plan_unskip,
   125→        )
   126→
   127→        dispatch = {
   128→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   129→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   130→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   131→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   132→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   133→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   134→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   135→        }
   136→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   140→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   141→
   142→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   143→        return
   144→
   145→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   146→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   147→
   148→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   149→        return
   150→
   151→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   152→
   153→
   154→# Backwards-compatible alias
   155→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   156→
   157→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]

1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
    40→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    41→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    42→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    46→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    47→        plan_data = plan
    48→
    49→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster
    50→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    51→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    52→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    53→        if active_cluster:
    54→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    55→
    56→    queue = build_work_queue(
    57→        state,
    58→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    59→            tier=tier_filter,
    60→            count=None,
    61→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    62→            status="open",
    63→            include_subjective=True,
    64→            plan=plan_data,
    65→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
    66→            cluster=effective_cluster,
    67→            collapse_clusters=True,
    68→        ),
    69→    )
    70→    items = queue.get("items", [])
    71→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    72→
    73→    # Header
    74→    total = len(items)
    75→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    76→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    77→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    78→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    79→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    80→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts})", "bold"))
    81→    else:
    82→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    83→
    84→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    85→    if focus:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    87→
    88→    if not include_skipped and skipped_count == 0:
    89→        # Check plan for skipped items not in queue
    90→        plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    91→        if plan_skipped_count:
    92→            print(colorize(
    93→                f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    94→                " hidden \u2014 use --include-skipped)",
    95→                "dim",
    96→            ))
    97→
    98→    if not items:
    99→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    # Determine which items to show
   103→    display_items = items
   104→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   105→        display_items = items[:top]
   106→
   107→    # Build rows
   108→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   109→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   110→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   111→        pos = str(idx)
   112→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   113→
   114→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   115→            tier_str = "T1"
   116→            detector = "synthesis"
   117→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   118→            n = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   119→            summary = f"[synthesis] {n} findings need integration"
   120→            cluster_name = ""
   121→        elif kind == "epic":
   122→            tier_str = "T1"
   123→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   124→            direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   125→            fcount = len(detail.get("finding_ids", []))
   126→            status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   127→            detector = "epic"
   128→            summary = f"[{direction}] [{fcount}] [{status}] {detail.get('thesis', '')}"
   129→            cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   130→        elif kind == "cluster":
   131→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
   132→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   133→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   134→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   135→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   136→        else:
   137→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   138→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   139→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   140→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   141→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   142→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   143→
   144→        suffix = ""
   145→        if item.get("plan_skipped"):
   146→            suffix = " [skip]"
   147→
   148→        summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
   149→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   150→
   151→    print()
   152→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   153→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   154→
   155→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   156→        remaining = len(items) - top
   157→        print(colorize(
   158→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   159→        ))
   160→    print()
   161→
   162→
   163→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   164→

1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 3
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}
    12→
    13→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = frozenset({
    14→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    15→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    16→})
    17→
    18→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    19→
    20→
    21→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    22→    finding_id: Required[str]
    23→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    24→    reason: str | None
    25→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    26→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    27→    created_at: str
    28→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    29→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    30→
    31→
    32→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    33→    finding_id: Required[str]
    34→    description: str | None
    35→    note: str | None
    36→    cluster: str | None
    37→    created_at: str
    38→    updated_at: str
    39→
    40→
    41→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    42→    name: Required[str]

    44→    finding_ids: list[str]

    46→    updated_at: str
    47→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    48→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)

    50→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    51→

    53→class Epic(TypedDict, total=False):
    54→    name: Required[str]
    55→    thesis: Required[str]
    56→    direction: Required[str]  # One of VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS
    57→    root_cause: str
    58→    finding_ids: list[str]
    59→    supersedes: list[str]
    60→    dismissed: list[str]
    61→    agent_safe: bool
    62→    dependency_order: int

    64→    source_clusters: list[str]
    65→    status: str  # "pending" | "in_progress" | "completed"
    66→    created_at: str
    67→    updated_at: str
    68→    synthesis_version: int
    69→    auto: bool  # Always True (LLM-generated)
    70→

    72→class EpicSynthesisMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
    73→    synthesized_ids: list[str]
    74→    last_run: str
    75→    version: int
    76→    dismissed_ids: list[str]
    77→    finding_snapshot_hash: str

    79→    trigger: str

    82→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    83→    original_id: Required[str]
    84→    original_detector: str
    85→    original_file: str
    86→    original_summary: str
    87→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    88→    superseded_at: str
    89→    remapped_to: str | None
    90→    candidates: list[str]
    91→    note: str | None
    92→
    93→
    94→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    95→    version: Required[int]
    96→    created: Required[str]
    97→    updated: Required[str]
    98→    queue_order: list[str]
    99→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   100→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   101→    active_cluster: str | None
   102→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   103→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   104→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   105→    epics: dict[str, Epic]
   106→    epic_synthesis_meta: EpicSynthesisMeta
   107→
   108→
   109→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   110→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   111→    now = utc_now()
   112→    return {
   113→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   114→        "created": now,
   115→        "updated": now,
   116→        "queue_order": [],
   117→        "deferred": [],
   118→        "skipped": {},
   119→        "active_cluster": None,
   120→        "overrides": {},
   121→        "clusters": {},
   122→        "superseded": {},
   123→        "epics": {},
   124→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   125→    }
   126→
   127→
   128→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   129→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   130→
   131→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
   132→    """
   133→    defaults = empty_plan()
   134→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   135→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   136→
   137→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   138→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   139→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   140→        plan["deferred"] = []
   141→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   142→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   143→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   144→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   145→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   146→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   147→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   148→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   149→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epics"), dict):
   150→        plan["epics"] = {}
   151→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   152→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   153→
   154→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   155→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   156→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   157→    if deferred:
   158→        now = utc_now()
   159→        for fid in list(deferred):
   160→            if fid not in skipped:
   161→                skipped[fid] = {
   162→                    "finding_id": fid,
   163→                    "kind": "temporary",
   164→                    "reason": None,
   165→                    "note": None,
   166→                    "attestation": None,
   167→                    "created_at": now,
   168→                    "review_after": None,
   169→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   170→                }
   171→        deferred.clear()
   172→
   173→    # Normalize cluster fields
   174→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   175→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   176→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   177→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   178→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   179→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   180→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   181→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   182→
   183→
   184→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   185→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   186→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   187→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   188→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   189→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   190→
   191→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   192→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   193→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   194→    if overlap:
   195→        raise ValueError(
   196→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   197→        )
   198→
   199→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   200→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   201→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   202→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   203→            raise ValueError(
   204→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   205→            )
   206→
   207→
   208→__all__ = [
   209→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   210→    "Epic",
   211→    "EpicSynthesisMeta",
   212→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   213→    "Cluster",
   214→    "ItemOverride",
   215→    "PlanModel",
   216→    "SkipEntry",
   217→    "SupersededEntry",
   218→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   219→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   220→    "empty_plan",
   221→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   222→    "validate_plan",
   223→]
   224→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   105→    plan: PlanModel,
   106→    state: StateModel,
   107→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   108→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   109→
   110→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   111→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   112→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   113→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   114→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   115→    """
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   118→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   119→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   120→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   121→
   122→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   123→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   124→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   125→        fid for fid in order
   126→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   127→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   128→        and fid not in stale_ids
   129→    ]
   130→    for fid in to_remove:
   131→        order.remove(fid)
   132→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   133→
   134→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs to front ----------------------------
   135→    existing = set(order)
   136→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   137→        if uid not in existing:
   138→            order.insert(0, uid)
   139→            result.injected.append(uid)
   140→
   141→    return result
   142→
   143→
   144→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   145→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   146→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147→
   148→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   149→    plan: PlanModel,
   150→    state: StateModel,
   151→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   152→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   153→
   154→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   155→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   156→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   157→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   158→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   159→    """
   160→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   161→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   162→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   163→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   164→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   165→
   166→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   167→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   168→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   169→        fid for fid in order
   170→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   171→        and fid not in stale_ids
   172→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   173→    ]
   174→    for fid in to_remove:
   175→        order.remove(fid)
   176→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   179→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   180→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   181→        existing = set(order)
   182→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   183→            if sid not in existing:
   184→                order.append(sid)
   185→                result.injected.append(sid)
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→@dataclass
   191→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   192→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   193→
   194→    injected: bool = False
   195→    pruned: bool = False
   196→
   197→    @property
   198→    def changes(self) -> int:
   199→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   200→
   201→
   202→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   203→    """Hash of sorted open review/concerns finding IDs."""
   204→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   205→    ids = sorted(
   206→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   207→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   208→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   209→    )
   210→    if not ids:
   211→        return ""
   212→    return hashlib.md5("|".join(ids).encode()).hexdigest()
   213→
   214→
   215→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   216→    plan: PlanModel,
   217→    state: StateModel,
   218→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   219→    """Inject/prune synthesis::pending based on review finding drift.
   220→
   221→    Triggers when open review/concerns findings exist AND the snapshot
   222→    hash differs from the last-synthesized hash.
   223→    """
   224→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   225→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   226→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   227→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   228→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   229→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   230→    has_synthesis = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   231→
   232→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   233→        # Need synthesis — inject if not present
   234→        if not has_synthesis:
   235→            order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
   236→            result.injected = True
   237→    else:
   238→        # Synthesis up to date or no review findings — prune if present
   239→        if has_synthesis:
   240→            order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   241→            result.pruned = True
   242→
   243→    return result
   244→
   245→
   246→__all__ = [
   247→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   248→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   249→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   250→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   251→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   252→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   253→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   254→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   255→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   256→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   257→]
   258→

    34→
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def _status_next_command(narrative: dict) -> str:
    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"
    71→
    72→
    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    stats: dict,
    77→    by_tier: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],

   100→            "objective_score": objective_score,
   101→            "strict_score": strict_score,

   108→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   109→            "by_tier": by_tier,

   111→            "suppression": suppression,

   118→            **({"plan": {

   122→                "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   123→                "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   124→            }} if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters") or plan.get("skipped")) else {}),
   125→        }

   152→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   153→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   154→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)

   163→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   164→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   165→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   166→        state,
