```
   170→
   171→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   172→    dim_scores: dict,
   173→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   174→) -> str | None:
   175→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   176→    lowest_name = None
   177→    lowest_score = 101.0
   178→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   179→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   180→        if not ds:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   183→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   184→            lowest_score = strict_val
   185→            lowest_name = dim.name
   186→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   187→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   188→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   189→            lowest_score = strict_val
   190→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   191→    return lowest_name
   192→
   193→
   194→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   195→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   196→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   197→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   198→        return {}
   199→
   200→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for finding in findings.values():
   202→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   203→            continue
   204→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   205→            continue
   206→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   207→        dimension = ""
   208→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   209→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   210→        if not dimension:
   211→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   212→        if not dimension:
   213→            continue
   214→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   215→    return counts
   216→
   217→
   218→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   219→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   222→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   223→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   224→        if not ds:
   225→            continue
   226→        score_val = ds["score"]
   227→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   228→        checks = ds["checks"]
   229→
   230→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   231→
   232→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   233→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   234→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   235→        print(
   236→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   237→        )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   241→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   242→    *,
   243→    lowest_name: str | None,
   244→    bar_len: int,
   245→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   248→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   249→        return
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   253→            "dim",
   254→        )
   255→    )
   256→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   257→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   258→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   259→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   260→        tier = 4
   261→
   262→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   263→
   264→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   265→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   266→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   267→        placeholder_tag = (
   268→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   269→        )
   270→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   271→        cli_keys = [
   272→            str(key).strip()
   273→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   274→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   275→        ]
   276→        if dim_key:
   277→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   278→        elif cli_keys:
   279→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   280→        else:
   281→            issue_count = 0
   282→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   283→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   284→        print(
   285→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   286→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   287→        )
   288→

   290→def _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective: list[dict], state: dict | None = None) -> None:
   291→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   292→    print(

   294→    )

   327→    print()

   352→def show_focus_suggestion(
   353→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   354→) -> None:
   355→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   356→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   357→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   358→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   359→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   360→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   361→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   362→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   363→        print(
   364→            colorize(
   365→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   366→                "cyan",
   367→            )
   368→        )
   369→        print()
   370→        return
   371→
   372→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   373→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   374→    if not lowest_name:
   375→        return
   376→
   377→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   378→    lowest_kind = None
   379→    lowest_score = 101.0
   380→    lowest_issues = 0
   381→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   382→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   383→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   384→            if ds:
   385→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   386→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   387→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   388→            break
   389→    else:
   390→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   391→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   392→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   394→                lowest_issues = 0
   395→                break
   396→
   397→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   398→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   399→            print(
   400→                colorize(
   401→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   402→                    "cyan",
   403→                )
   404→            )
   405→            print()
   406→            return
   407→
   408→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   409→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   410→        if target_dim:
   411→            impact = 0.0
   412→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   413→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   414→                    {
   415→                        k: {
   416→                            "score": v["score"],
   417→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   418→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   419→                        }
   420→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   421→                        if "score" in v
   422→                    },
   423→                    potentials,
   424→                    det,
   425→                    lowest_issues,
   426→                )
   427→                if impact > 0:
   428→                    break
   429→
   430→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   431→            print(
   432→                colorize(
   433→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   434→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   435→                    "cyan",
   436→                )
   437→            )
   438→            print()
   439→

   493→def _collect_structural_areas(
   494→    state: dict,
   495→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   496→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   497→
   498→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   499→    """
   500→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   501→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   502→    )
   503→
   504→    structural = [
   505→        f
   506→        for f in findings.values()
   507→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   508→    ]
   509→
   510→    if len(structural) < 5:
   511→        return None
   512→
   513→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   514→    for f in structural:
   515→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   516→        areas[area].append(f)
   517→
   518→    if len(areas) < 2:
   519→        return None
   520→
   521→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   522→
   523→
   524→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   525→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   526→    rows = []
   527→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   528→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   529→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   530→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   531→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   532→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   533→        rows.append(
   534→            [
   535→                area,
   536→                str(len(area_findings)),
   537→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   538→                str(open_count),
   539→                str(debt_count),
   540→                str(weight),
   541→            ]
   542→        )
   543→    return rows
   544→
   545→
   546→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   547→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   548→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   549→    if remaining > 0:
   550→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   551→
   552→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   553→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   554→    print(
   555→        colorize(
   556→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   557→        )
   558→    )
   559→    print(
   560→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   561→    )
   562→    print()
   563→
   564→
   565→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   566→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   567→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   568→    if sorted_areas is None:
   569→        return
   570→
   571→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   572→    print(
   573→        colorize(
   574→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   575→            "dim",
   576→        )
   577→    )
   578→    print()
   579→
   580→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   581→    print_table(
   582→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   583→    )
   584→
   585→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   586→
   587→
   588→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   589→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   590→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   591→    review_open = [
   592→        f
   593→        for f in findings.values()
   594→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   595→    ]
   596→    if not review_open:

   598→    uninvestigated = sum(
   599→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   600→    )

   602→    if uninvestigated:

   604→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   605→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   606→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
```

```
    11→def print_agent_plan(

    13→    *,
    14→    next_command: str | None = None,
    15→    header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:",

    34→
    35→def _print_plan_agent_block(plan: dict, *, header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:") -> None:
    36→    """Render the living plan as the agent plan block."""
    37→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    38→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    39→    skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    42→    print(colorize(f"  Living plan active: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.", "dim"))
    43→    if active:
    44→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    45→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    46→        print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).", "dim"))
    47→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    48→    print(colorize("  View plan: `desloppify plan`", "dim"))
    49→
    50→
    51→def print_replacement_groups(
    52→    groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],
    53→    *,
    54→    title: str,
    55→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] = rel,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    """Print grouped old→new replacement lines by file."""
    58→    if not groups:
    59→        return
    60→    print(colorize(title, "cyan"))
    61→    for filepath, replacements in sorted(groups.items()):
    62→        print(f"    {rel_fn(filepath)}:")
    63→        for old, new in replacements:
    64→            print(f"      {old}  →  {new}")
    65→    print()
    66→
    67→
    68→def print_ranked_actions(
    69→    actions: list[dict], *, limit: int = 3, plan: dict | None = None
    70→) -> bool:
    71→    """Print the highest-impact narrative actions and return True when shown."""
    72→    ranked = sorted(
    73→        [action for action in actions if int(action.get("count", 0)) > 0],
    74→        key=lambda action: (
    75→            -float(action.get("impact", 0.0)),
    76→            -int(action.get("count", 0)),
    77→            int(action.get("priority", 999)),
    78→        ),
    79→    )
    80→    if not ranked:
    81→        return False
    82→    has_plan = plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"))
    83→    label = "Score context:" if has_plan else "Biggest things impacting score:"
    84→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "cyan"))
    85→    for action in ranked[:limit]:
    86→        detector = action.get("detector", "unknown")
    87→        count = int(action.get("count", 0))
    88→        cluster_count = action.get("cluster_count")
    89→        if cluster_count:
    90→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open in {cluster_count} cluster(s) — `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    91→        else:
    92→            command = action.get("command", "desloppify next")
    93→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`", "dim"))
    94→    return True
    95→
    96→
    97→__all__ = [
    98→    "print_agent_plan",
    99→    "print_ranked_actions",
   100→    "print_replacement_groups",
   101→]
   102→
```

```
1→"""Skill-document versioning and install metadata helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import get_project_root
     9→
    10→# Bump this integer whenever docs/SKILL.md changes in a way that agents
    11→# should pick up (new commands, changed workflows, removed sections).
    12→SKILL_VERSION = 2
    13→
    14→SKILL_VERSION_RE = re.compile(r"<!--\s*desloppify-skill-version:\s*(\d+)\s*-->")
    15→SKILL_OVERLAY_RE = re.compile(r"<!--\s*desloppify-overlay:\s*(\w+)\s*-->")
    16→
    17→SKILL_BEGIN = "<!-- desloppify-begin -->"
    18→SKILL_END = "<!-- desloppify-end -->"
    19→
    20→# Locations where the skill doc might be installed, relative to project root.
    21→SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS = (
    22→    ".claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md",
    23→    ".opencode/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md",
    24→    "AGENTS.md",
    25→    "CLAUDE.md",
    26→    ".cursor/rules/desloppify.md",
    27→    ".github/copilot-instructions.md",
    28→)
    29→
    30→# Interface name → (target file, overlay filename, dedicated).
    31→# Dedicated files are overwritten entirely; shared files get section replacement.
    32→SKILL_TARGETS: dict[str, tuple[str, str, bool]] = {
    33→    "claude": (".claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md", "CLAUDE", True),
    34→    # OpenCode support added with thanks to @H3xKatana.
    35→    "opencode": (".opencode/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md", "OPENCODE", True),
    36→    "codex": ("AGENTS.md", "CODEX", False),
    37→    "cursor": (".cursor/rules/desloppify.md", "CURSOR", True),
    38→    "copilot": (".github/copilot-instructions.md", "COPILOT", False),
    39→    "windsurf": ("AGENTS.md", "WINDSURF", False),
    40→    "gemini": ("AGENTS.md", "GEMINI", False),
    41→}
    42→
    43→
    44→@dataclass
    45→class SkillInstall:
    46→    """Detected skill document installation."""
    47→
    48→    rel_path: str
    49→    version: int
    50→    overlay: str | None
    51→    stale: bool
    52→
    53→
    54→def find_all_installed_skills() -> list[SkillInstall]:
    55→    """Find all installed skill documents across all search paths."""
    56→    project_root = get_project_root()
    57→    results: list[SkillInstall] = []
    58→    for rel_path in SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS:
    59→        full = project_root / rel_path
    60→        if not full.is_file():
    61→            continue
    62→        try:
    63→            content = full.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    64→        except OSError:
    65→            continue
    66→        version_match = SKILL_VERSION_RE.search(content)
    67→        if not version_match:
    68→            continue
    69→        installed_version = int(version_match.group(1))
    70→        overlay_match = SKILL_OVERLAY_RE.search(content)
    71→        overlay = overlay_match.group(1) if overlay_match else None
    72→        results.append(SkillInstall(
    73→            rel_path=rel_path,
    74→            version=installed_version,
    75→            overlay=overlay,
    76→            stale=installed_version < SKILL_VERSION,
    77→        ))
    78→    return results
    79→
    80→
    81→def find_installed_skill() -> SkillInstall | None:
    82→    """Find first installed skill document metadata, or None."""
    83→    installs = find_all_installed_skills()
    84→    return installs[0] if installs else None
    85→
    86→
    87→def check_skill_version() -> str | None:
    88→    """Return a warning if any installed skill doc is outdated."""
    89→    installs = find_all_installed_skills()
    90→    stale = [i for i in installs if i.stale]
    91→    if not stale:
    92→        return None
    93→    if len(stale) == 1:
    94→        return (
    95→            f"Your desloppify skill document is outdated "
    96→            f"(v{stale[0].version}, current v{SKILL_VERSION}). "
    97→            "Run: desloppify update-skill"
    98→        )
    99→    paths = ", ".join(i.rel_path for i in stale)
   100→    return (
   101→        f"{len(stale)} skill documents are outdated "
   102→        f"(current v{SKILL_VERSION}): {paths}. "
   103→        "Run: desloppify update-skill"
   104→    )
   105→
   106→
   107→__all__ = [
   108→    "SKILL_VERSION",
   109→    "SKILL_VERSION_RE",
   110→    "SKILL_OVERLAY_RE",
   111→    "SKILL_BEGIN",
   112→    "SKILL_END",
   113→    "SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS",
   114→    "SKILL_TARGETS",
   115→    "SkillInstall",
   116→    "find_all_installed_skills",
   117→    "find_installed_skill",
   118→    "check_skill_version",
   119→]
   120→
```

```
1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import render as next_render_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    15→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    16→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    19→    QueueBuildOptions,
    20→    build_work_queue,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    23→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness, check_tool_staleness
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    26→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    76→    if config_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→
    79→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    80→
    81→
    82→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    83→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    84→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    85→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    86→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    87→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    88→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    89→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    90→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    91→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    92→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    93→
    94→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    95→
    96→    # Load the living plan
    97→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    98→    plan = load_plan()
    99→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   100→    if (
   101→        plan.get("queue_order")
   102→        or plan.get("overrides")
   103→        or plan.get("clusters")
   104→    ):
   105→        plan_data = plan
   106→
   107→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   109→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   110→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   111→        if active_cluster:
   112→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   113→
   114→    queue = build_work_queue(
   115→        state,
   116→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   117→            tier=tier,
   118→            count=count,
   119→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   120→            scope=scope,
   121→            status=status,
   122→            include_subjective=True,
   123→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   124→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   125→            explain=explain,
   126→            plan=plan_data,
   127→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   128→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   129→        ),
   130→    )
   131→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   132→
   133→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   134→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   135→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   136→        state,
   137→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   141→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   142→    )
   143→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   144→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   145→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   147→        state,
   148→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   149→    )
   150→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   151→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   152→    ]
   153→    write_query(payload)
   154→
   155→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   156→    if output_file:
   157→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   158→            output_file,
   159→            payload,
   160→            len(items),
   161→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   162→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   163→        ):
   164→            return
   165→        raise SystemExit(1)
   166→
   167→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   171→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   172→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   176→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   177→        state.get("findings", {}),
   178→        state.get("scan_path"),
   179→    )
   180→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   183→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   184→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   186→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   187→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   188→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   189→    )
   190→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   191→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   192→        state,
   193→        dim_scores,
   194→        findings_scoped,
   195→        strict_score=strict_score,
   196→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   197→    )
   198→    print()
   199→
   200→
   201→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   202→
```

```
431→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   432→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   433→
   434→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   435→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   436→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   437→    """
   438→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   439→    found = 0
   440→
   441→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   442→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   443→    for fid in finding_ids:
   444→        was_present = False
   445→        if fid in order:
   446→            order.remove(fid)
   447→            was_present = True
   448→        if fid in skipped:
   449→            skipped.pop(fid)
   450→            was_present = True
   451→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   452→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   453→            if fid in ids:
   454→                ids.remove(fid)
   455→                was_present = True
   456→        if was_present:
   457→            found += 1
   458→
   459→    return found
   460→
   461→
   462→__all__ = [
   463→    "add_to_cluster",
   464→    "annotate_finding",
   465→    "clear_focus",
   466→    "create_cluster",
   467→    "delete_cluster",
   468→    "describe_finding",
   469→    "move_cluster",
   470→    "move_items",
```

```
    10→
    11→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    12→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    13→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    14→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    15→        print(f"  {fid}")
    16→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    17→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    18→
    19→
    20→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    21→    state: dict,
    22→    *,
    23→    status: str,
    24→    resolved_count: int,
    25→) -> None:
    26→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    27→        return
    28→    wontfix_count = sum(
    29→        1 for finding in state["findings"].values() if finding["status"] == "wontfix"
    30→    )
    31→    actionable = sum(
    32→        1
    33→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    34→        if finding["status"]
    35→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    36→    )
    37→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    38→    print(
    39→        colorize(
    40→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    41→            "yellow",
    42→        )
    43→    )
    44→    print(
    45→        colorize(
    46→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    47→            "dim",
    48→        )
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    53→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    54→        return ""
    55→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    56→
    57→
    58→def _print_score_movement(
    59→    *,
    60→    status: str,
    61→    prev_overall: float | None,
    62→    prev_objective: float | None,
    63→    prev_strict: float | None,
    64→    prev_verified: float | None,
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    67→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    68→) -> None:
    69→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    70→    if (
    71→        new.overall is None
    72→        or new.objective is None
    73→        or new.strict is None
    74→        or new.verified is None
    75→    ):
    76→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    77→        return
    78→
    79→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    80→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    81→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    82→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    83→    print(
    84→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    85→        + colorize(
    86→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    87→            "dim",
    88→        )
    89→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    90→        + colorize(
    91→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    92→        )
    93→    )
    94→    if target_strict is not None:
    95→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import _print_strict_target_nudge
    96→
    97→        _print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
    98→    if status == "wontfix":
    99→        strict_gap = round(new.overall - new.strict, 1)
   100→        if strict_gap > 0:
   101→            print(
   102→                colorize(
   103→                    f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
   104→                    f"Current gap: overall {new.overall:.1f} vs strict {new.strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
   105→                    "yellow",
   106→                )
   107→            )
   108→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
   109→        print(
   110→            colorize(
   111→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
   112→                "yellow",
   113→            )
   114→        )
   115→        print(
   116→            colorize(
   117→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
   118→                "dim",
   119→            )
   120→        )
   121→    elif status == "fixed":
   122→        print(
   123→            colorize(
   124→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
   125→                "yellow",
   126→            )
   127→        )
   128→
   129→
   130→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   131→    *,
   132→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   133→    state: dict,
   134→    all_resolved: list[str],
   135→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   136→) -> None:
   137→    has_review = any(
   138→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   139→        for fid in all_resolved
   140→    )
   141→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   142→        return
   143→
   144→    stale_dims = sorted(
   145→        dim
   146→        for dim in {
   147→            str(
   148→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   149→            ).strip()
   150→            for fid in all_resolved
   151→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   152→        }
   153→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   154→    )
   155→    if not stale_dims:
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   159→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   160→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   161→    print(
   162→        colorize(
   163→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   164→            "yellow",
   165→        )
   166→    )
   167→    print(
   168→        colorize(
   169→            "  Next subjective step: "
   170→            + f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(stale_dims)}`",
   171→            "dim",
   172→        )
   173→    )
   174→
   175→
   176→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   177→    remaining = sum(
   178→        1
   179→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   180→        if finding["status"] == "open"
   181→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   182→    )
   183→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   184→    if remaining > 0:
   185→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   186→        print(
   187→            colorize(
   188→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   189→                "dim",
   190→            )
   191→        )
   192→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   193→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   194→    print()
   195→    return next_command
   196→
```

```python
            # Report epic progress and purge from epic finding_ids
            epics = plan.get("epics", {})
            for epic_name, epic in epics.items():
                epic_fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
                hits = [fid for fid in all_resolved if fid in epic_fids]
                if hits:
                    for fid in hits:
                        epic_fids.remove(fid)
                    remaining = len(epic_fids)
                    if remaining == 0:
                        print(colorize(f"  Epic {epic_name}: completed!", "green"))
                        epic["status"] = "completed"
                    else:
                        print(colorize(
                            f"  Epic {epic_name}: {remaining} finding(s) remaining.",
                            "dim",
                        ))
```

Note: This must be placed BEFORE `save_plan(plan)` (line 99), so we need to restructure slightly. The current code saves inside the `if purged:` block. The epic progress code should run regardless of whether `purged > 0` (a finding might be in an epic but not in queue_order). So the structure becomes:

```python
        if has_living_plan():
            plan = load_plan()
            purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
            # Report and purge from epic finding_ids
            epics = plan.get("epics", {})
            epic_changed = False
            for epic_name, epic in epics.items():
                epic_fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
                hits = [fid for fid in all_resolved if fid in epic_fids]
                if hits:
                    for fid in hits:
                        epic_fids.remove(fid)
                    epic_changed = True
                    remaining = len(epic_fids)
                    if remaining == 0:
                        print(colorize(f"  Epic {epic_name}: completed!", "green"))
                        epic["status"] = "completed"
                    else:
                        print(colorize(
                            f"  Epic {epic_name}: {remaining} finding(s) remaining.",
                            "dim",
                        ))
            if purged or epic_changed:
                save_plan(plan)
                if purged:
                    print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
```

**Lines changed:** ~16 (replaces existing 4-line block at lines 96-102).

```python
    if clusters:
        manual = {n: c for n, c in clusters.items() if not c.get("auto")}

        for name, cluster in manual.items():
```

#### 6. `cluster show` -- Display per-member annotations

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py`

**What to change:** In `_print_cluster_member()` (lines 117-129), after printing summary, check if the finding ID has an override with description or note in the plan.

The simplest approach: pass overrides into `_cmd_cluster_show` and thread through to `_print_cluster_member`.

**Change `_print_cluster_member` signature and body:**
```python
def _print_cluster_member(idx: int, fid: str, finding: dict | None, override: dict | None = None) -> None:
    """Print a single cluster member line with optional finding details."""
    print(f"    {idx}. {fid}")
    if not finding:
        pass  # continue to override check
    else:
        file = finding.get("file", "")
        lines = finding.get("detail", {}).get("lines", [])
        line_str = f" at lines: {', '.join(str(ln) for ln in lines)}" if lines else ""
        if file:
            print(colorize(f"       File: {file}{line_str}", "dim"))
        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
        if summary:
            print(colorize(f"       {summary}", "dim"))
    if override:
        if override.get("description"):
            print(colorize(f"       Description: {override['description']}", "cyan"))
        if override.get("note"):
            print(colorize(f"       Note: {override['note']}", "dim"))
```

**And in `_cmd_cluster_show()`, line 165-167, pass overrides:**
```python

        for idx, fid in enumerate(finding_ids, 1):
```

**Exact code to insert after the epic progress block, still inside the `try` block:**
```python
            # Nudge about synthesis staleness when review findings are resolved

                for fid in all_resolved
```

**Also in `cmd_plan_skip` (same file: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`):**

After `save_plan(plan)` at line 151, add the same check. But `cmd_plan_skip` doesn't have `state["findings"]` loaded with full detail. The `state` from `command_runtime` does have findings. So:

**Insert after line 154 in override_handlers.py:**
```python
    # Nudge about synthesis staleness when review findings are skipped
    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    has_review = any(
        findings.get(fid, {}).get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
        for fid in finding_ids
```

### Implementation Order

1. **Skill version bump + SKILL.md** (items 1) -- must come first since all agents will reload it on next scan
2. **Epic progress in resolve** (item 4) -- highest workflow impact
3. **Scan LLM block strategy context** (item 2) -- agents need this after each scan
4. **Synthesis visibility under focus** (item 3) -- prevents lost awareness
5. **Synthesis staleness nudge** (item 7) -- closes the loop on resolve flow
6. **Plan show collapse** (item 5) -- readability
7. **Cluster show annotations** (item 6) -- completeness
8. **Queue column width** (item 8) -- trivial
9. **Status epic summary** (item 9) -- supplementary

### Testing Strategy

After each change:
1. Run `python -m pytest desloppify/tests/ -q` to confirm no regressions
2. Run `python -m desloppify --lang python scan --path desloppify` to test scan LLM block
3. Run `python -m desloppify plan show` to test collapsed clusters
4. Run `python -m desloppify next` with and without focus to test synthesis visibility
5. Run `python -m desloppify plan done <id> --note "test" --attest "..."` to test epic progress
